Runtime configuration and databases were not collected.

FILE: /opt/si-equity-buy-worker/anti_churn_v1.py
SHA256: ec94a6652b98f5534332aaa9cbe8a9610ea98c3cb6e300db3136feb432898c50

"""SI_EQUITY_ANTI_CHURN_V1: additional BUY restrictions only."""
import json
import math
import time
from decimal import Decimal
from pathlib import Path

CONFIG = Path("/opt/si-equity-buy-worker/anti_churn_v1.json")

class EntryBlocked(RuntimeError):
    pass

def number(value):
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise ValueError("Boolean number")
    result = Decimal(str(value))
    if not result.is_finite():
        raise ValueError("Nonfinite number")
    return result

def settings():
    data = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text())
    if set(data) != {"cooldown_minutes", "recovery_bars"}:
        raise ValueError("Invalid anti-churn configuration")
    if (
        type(data["cooldown_minutes"]) is not int
        or not 1 <= data["cooldown_minutes"] <= 1440
        or type(data["recovery_bars"]) is not int
        or not 2 <= data["recovery_bars"] <= 6
    ):
        raise ValueError("Anti-churn settings out of range")
    return data

def recovery(rows, now, required, after=None):
    if not rows:
        raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_NO_PRICE_HISTORY")

    points = []
    for row in rows:
        timestamp = float(row["t"])
        price = number(row["price"])
        if (
            not math.isfinite(timestamp)
            or timestamp > now + 2
            or price <= 0
        ):
            raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_INVALID_PRICE_HISTORY")
        points.append((timestamp, price))
    points.sort()

    if now - points[-1][0] > 25:
        raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_STALE_PRICE_HISTORY")

    width = 300
    current_start = int(now // width) * width
    groups = {}
    for timestamp, price in points:
        start = int(timestamp // width) * width
        groups.setdefault(start, []).append((timestamp, price))

    bars = []
    for offset in range(required, 0, -1):
        start = current_start - offset * width
        observations = groups.get(start, [])

        # Require continuous sampling across each completed bar.
        if (
            len(observations) < 12
            or observations[0][0] - start > 25
            or start + width - observations[-1][0] > 25
            or any(
                observations[i][0] - observations[i-1][0] > 25
                for i in range(1, len(observations))
            )
        ):
            raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_INCOMPLETE_RECOVERY_BARS")

        if after is not None and start < after:
            raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_WAIT_POST_EXIT_BARS")

        prices = [price for _, price in observations]
        bars.append({
            "low": min(prices),
            "close": prices[-1],
        })

    if any(
        bars[i]["close"] <= bars[i-1]["close"]
        or bars[i]["low"] < bars[i-1]["low"]
        for i in range(1, len(bars))
    ):
        raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_FALLING_OR_NO_RECOVERY")

    current = groups.get(current_start, [])
    if current and min(price for _, price in current) < bars[-1]["low"]:
        raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_CURRENT_BAR_NEW_LOW")

    if points[-1][1] < bars[-1]["close"]:
        raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_RECOVERY_NOT_HOLDING")

def check(db, symbol, terminal, ignore_ref=None):
    try:
        return _check(db, symbol, terminal, ignore_ref)
    except EntryBlocked:
        raise
    except Exception as error:
        raise EntryBlocked(
            "ANTI_CHURN_STATE_UNAVAILABLE:" + type(error).__name__
        ) from error


def _si_next_day_add_guard_v2(db, symbol, orders, qty, now):
    if qty <= 0:
        return
    from datetime import datetime
    from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
    today = datetime.fromtimestamp(
        now, ZoneInfo("America/New_York")
    ).date().isoformat()
    signature = json.dumps([
        (row["ref"], str(number(row["filled"])))
        for row in orders
        if row["side"] == "buy" and number(row["filled"]) > 0
    ])
    db.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS anti_churn_buy_day_v2("
        "symbol TEXT PRIMARY KEY,signature TEXT NOT NULL,"
        "observed_day TEXT NOT NULL)"
    )
    saved = db.execute(
        "SELECT signature,observed_day FROM anti_churn_buy_day_v2 "
        "WHERE symbol=?", (symbol,)
    ).fetchone()
    if saved is None or saved[0] != signature:
        db.execute(
            "INSERT INTO anti_churn_buy_day_v2 VALUES(?,?,?) "
            "ON CONFLICT(symbol) DO UPDATE SET "
            "signature=excluded.signature,observed_day=excluded.observed_day",
            (symbol, signature, today)
        )
        db.commit()
        raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_BUY_OBSERVED_TODAY")
    from datetime import date
    observed_day = date.fromisoformat(saved[1])
    if observed_day.isoformat() >= today:
        raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_BUY_OBSERVED_TODAY")

def _check(db, symbol, terminal, ignore_ref):
    cfg = settings()
    now = time.time()

    orders = db.execute(
        "SELECT ref,side,state,filled,created FROM intents "
        "WHERE symbol=? ORDER BY created,ref",
        (symbol,),
    ).fetchall()

    qty = Decimal(0)
    latest_sell = None
    unresolved = False

    for row in orders:
        fill = number(row["filled"])
        if fill < 0 or row["side"] not in ("buy", "sell"):
            raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_INVALID_FILL_LEDGER")

        if row["side"] == "buy":
            qty += fill
        else:
            qty -= fill
            if fill > 0:
                latest_sell = (
                    row["ref"] + ":" + format(fill, "f")
                )

        if qty < 0:
            raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_INVALID_OWNERSHIP")

        if (
            row["ref"] != ignore_ref
            and row["state"] not in set(terminal) | {"LOCAL_BLOCKED"}
        ):
            unresolved = True

    db.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS anti_churn_state_v1("
        "symbol TEXT PRIMARY KEY,"
        "sell_signature TEXT,"
        "exit_observed REAL)"
    )
    saved = db.execute(
        "SELECT sell_signature,exit_observed "
        "FROM anti_churn_state_v1 WHERE symbol=?",
        (symbol,),
    ).fetchone()

    exit_observed = saved["exit_observed"] if saved else None
    previous_signature = saved["sell_signature"] if saved else None

    # The ledger has no execution timestamp for each fill.
    # Start conservatively when a changed SELL fill is first observed.
    if latest_sell is not None and latest_sell != previous_signature:
        exit_observed = now
        db.execute(
            "INSERT INTO anti_churn_state_v1 VALUES(?,?,?) "
            "ON CONFLICT(symbol) DO UPDATE SET "
            "sell_signature=excluded.sell_signature,"
            "exit_observed=excluded.exit_observed",
            (symbol, latest_sell, exit_observed),
        )
    db.commit()

    if unresolved:
        raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_ORDER_UNRESOLVED")
    _si_next_day_add_guard_v2(db, symbol, orders, qty, now)

    if exit_observed is not None:
        if (
            not math.isfinite(float(exit_observed))
            or exit_observed > now + 2
        ):
            raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_INVALID_EXIT_TIMESTAMP")
        from datetime import datetime
        from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
        ny = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")
        if datetime.fromtimestamp(exit_observed, ny).date() >= datetime.fromtimestamp(now, ny).date():
            raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_SELL_OBSERVED_TODAY")
        if now - exit_observed < cfg["cooldown_minutes"] * 60:
            raise EntryBlocked("ANTI_CHURN_EXIT_COOLDOWN")

    rows = db.execute(
        "SELECT t,price FROM samples "
        "WHERE symbol=? AND t>=? ORDER BY t",
        (symbol, now - 3600),
    ).fetchall()

    recovery(rows, now, cfg["recovery_bars"], exit_observed)

========================================================================
FILE: /opt/si-equity-buy-worker/broker_link_offhours_v1.py
SHA256: cb6be147d6be2c26d400610bf54166f73f46ea751edf4085041b9648b6ef2d32
"""Off-hours broker review only. Never authorizes or submits orders."""
import asyncio
import fcntl
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import os
import tempfile
import time
from collections import Counter
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

ROOT = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-buy-worker")
CONFIG = Path("/etc/si-equity-buy-worker/config.json")
OUTPUT = ROOT / "broker-link-review.json"
ARCHIVE = ROOT / "research-history" / "broker-link"
NY = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")
MAX_RUN_SECONDS = 300
REQUEST_TIMEOUT = 45

def stamp():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def window_open():
    now = datetime.now(NY)
    if now.hour >= 20:
        return True
    # Leave a one-minute buffer before the 08:00 cutoff.
    return now.hour < 7 or (now.hour == 7 and now.minute < 59)

def config():
    cfg = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text())
    if (
        cfg.get("live_enabled") is not False
        or cfg.get("programmatic_execution_authorized") is not False
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("EXECUTION_FLAGS_MUST_REMAIN_FALSE")
    return cfg

def atomic_save(path, value):
    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".research-", dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as stream:
            os.fchmod(stream.fileno(), 0o600)
            json.dump(value, stream, separators=(",", ":"))
            stream.write("\n")
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary):
            os.unlink(temporary)

def archive_checkpoint(label):
    if not OUTPUT.exists():
        return
    ARCHIVE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
    raw = OUTPUT.read_bytes()
    json.loads(raw)
    name = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ")
    path = ARCHIVE / f"{name}-{label}.json"
    with path.open("xb") as stream:
        os.fchmod(stream.fileno(), 0o600)
        stream.write(raw)
        stream.flush()
        os.fsync(stream.fileno())

async def scan():
    cfg = config()
    review = json.loads((ROOT / "listing-screen-review.json").read_text())
    source_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        (ROOT / "listing-catalog.json").read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()

    if review.get("source_sha256") != source_hash:
        raise RuntimeError("CATALOG_CHANGED_REGENERATE_PRELIMINARY_REPORT")
    if review.get("entry_authorized") is not False:
        raise RuntimeError("INVALID_REVIEW_AUTHORIZATION")

    records = review.get("records_for_review")
    if not isinstance(records, list) or not records:
        raise RuntimeError("REVIEW_RECORDS_MISSING")
    if any(
        not isinstance(row, dict)
        or not isinstance(row.get("listing_symbol"), str)
        for row in records
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("INVALID_REVIEW_RECORDS")

    symbols = sorted({row["listing_symbol"] for row in records})
    identity = {
        "source_sha256": source_hash,
        "review_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps(records, sort_keys=True).encode()
        ).hexdigest(),
        "account_fingerprint": hashlib.sha256(
            cfg["account_number"].encode()
        ).hexdigest(),
    }

    if OUTPUT.exists():
        checkpoint = json.loads(OUTPUT.read_text())
        if checkpoint.get("identity") != identity:
            raise RuntimeError("CHECKPOINT_INPUT_CHANGED_EXISTING_DATA_PRESERVED")
        results = checkpoint.get("results_by_symbol")
        if (
            not isinstance(results, dict)
            or not set(results).issubset(set(symbols))
            or any(not isinstance(row, dict) for row in results.values())
        ):
            raise RuntimeError("INVALID_CHECKPOINT")
    else:
        checkpoint = {
            "identity": identity,
            "started_at": stamp(),
            "results_by_symbol": {},
        }
        results = checkpoint["results_by_symbol"]

    archive_checkpoint("before")
    todo = [s for s in symbols if s not in results]
    started = time.monotonic()

    if not todo:
        print("BROKER_REVIEW_ALREADY_COMPLETE_NOT_ENTRY_AUTHORIZED")
        return

    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        "equity_offhours_broker",
        "/opt/si-equity-buy-worker/equity_worker.py",
    )
    worker = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(worker)

    async def read(broker, name, args):
        if not window_open():
            raise RuntimeError("OFFHOURS_WINDOW_CLOSED")
        if name not in {"get_accounts", "get_equity_tradability"}:
            raise RuntimeError("READ_TOOL_NOT_ALLOWED")
        return await asyncio.wait_for(
            broker.call(name, args), timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT
        )

    try:
        if not window_open():
            raise RuntimeError("OFFHOURS_WINDOW_CLOSED")
        async with worker.MCPBroker(cfg) as broker:
            accounts = (await read(broker, "get_accounts", {})).get("accounts")
            if not isinstance(accounts, list):
                raise RuntimeError("INVALID_ACCOUNTS_RESPONSE")
            matches = [
                a for a in accounts if isinstance(a, dict)
                and a.get("account_number") == cfg["account_number"]
            ]
            if len(matches) != 1:
                raise RuntimeError("CONFIGURED_ACCOUNT_NOT_UNIQUE")
            account = matches[0]
            if (
                account.get("agentic_allowed") is not True
                or account.get("state") != "active"
                or account.get("deactivated") is not False
                or account.get("permanently_deactivated") is not False
            ):
                raise RuntimeError("CONFIGURED_ACCOUNT_NOT_ELIGIBLE")

            category = account.get("brokerage_account_type")
            if not isinstance(category, str) or not category:
                raise RuntimeError("ACCOUNT_CATEGORY_MISSING")
            previous = checkpoint.get("brokerage_account_type")
            if previous is not None and previous != category:
                raise RuntimeError("ACCOUNT_CATEGORY_CHANGED")
            checkpoint["brokerage_account_type"] = category

            for offset in range(0, len(todo), 10):
                if (
                    not window_open()
                    or time.monotonic() - started >= MAX_RUN_SECONDS
                ):
                    break

                current = config()
                if current["account_number"] != cfg["account_number"]:
                    raise RuntimeError("CONFIGURED_ACCOUNT_CHANGED")

                batch = todo[offset:offset + 10]
                data = await read(broker, "get_equity_tradability", {
                    "account_number": cfg["account_number"],
                    "symbols": batch,
                })
                rows = data.get("results")
                missing = data.get("not_found")
                rows = [] if rows is None else rows
                missing = [] if missing is None else missing
                if not isinstance(rows, list) or not isinstance(missing, list):
                    raise RuntimeError("INVALID_BATCH_RESPONSE")
                if any(
                    not isinstance(r, dict) or r.get("symbol") not in batch
                    for r in rows
                ) or any(s not in batch for s in missing):
                    raise RuntimeError("UNEXPECTED_BATCH_RESULT")

                checked_at = stamp()
                for symbol in batch:
                    found = [r for r in rows if r.get("symbol") == symbol]
                    row = found[0] if len(found) == 1 else {}
                    reasons = []
                    if symbol in missing:
                        reasons.append("BROKER_SYMBOL_NOT_FOUND")
                    if len(found) != 1:
                        reasons.append("RESULT_MISSING_OR_DUPLICATED")
                    else:
                        if row.get("tradeable") is not True:
                            reasons.append("NOT_TRADEABLE")
                        if row.get("state") != "active":
                            reasons.append("NOT_CONFIRMED_ACTIVE")
                        if row.get("fractional_tradability") != "tradable":
                            reasons.append("FRACTIONAL_NOT_TRADABLE")
                        halts = row.get("internal_halt_sessions")
                        if halts is not None and (
                            not isinstance(halts, list)
                            or any(not isinstance(x, str) for x in halts)
                        ):
                            reasons.append("INVALID_HALT_DATA")
                        elif "regular_hours" in (halts or []):
                            reasons.append("REGULAR_SESSION_HALTED")

                        typed = row.get("account_type_tradabilities")
                        if not isinstance(typed, list) or any(
                            not isinstance(x, dict) for x in typed
                        ):
                            reasons.append("ACCOUNT_CATEGORY_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE")
                        else:
                            matched = [
                                x for x in typed
                                if x.get("account_type") == category
                            ]
                            if len(matched) != 1:
                                reasons.append("ACCOUNT_CATEGORY_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE")
                            elif matched[0].get(
                                "account_type_tradability"
                            ) != "tradable":
                                reasons.append("ACCOUNT_CATEGORY_NOT_TRADABLE")

                    results[symbol] = {
                        "checked_at": checked_at,
                        "broker_name": row.get("name"),
                        "tradability_checks_passed": not reasons,
                        "hold_reasons": reasons,
                        "classification_verified": False,
                        "news_risk_verified": False,
                        "earnings_risk_verified": False,
                        "entry_authorized": False,
                    }

                checkpoint.update(
                    state="BROKER_REVIEW_COMPLETE_NOT_ENTRY_AUTHORIZED"
                    if len(results) == len(symbols)
                    else "BROKER_REVIEW_PARTIAL_NOT_ENTRY_AUTHORIZED",
                    updated_at=stamp(),
                    total_symbols=len(symbols),
                    checked_symbols=len(results),
                    entry_authorized=False,
                )
                atomic_save(OUTPUT, checkpoint)
                print(f"Checked {len(results)}/{len(symbols)}", flush=True)
                await asyncio.sleep(1)
    finally:
        archive_checkpoint("after")

    counts = Counter(
        "TRADABILITY_CHECKS_PASSED"
        if r.get("tradability_checks_passed") is True else "HELD"
        for r in results.values()
    )
    print(json.dumps({
        "state": checkpoint.get("state"),
        "checked_symbols": len(results),
        "remaining_symbols": len(symbols) - len(results),
        "summary": dict(counts),
        "entry_authorized": False,
    }, indent=2))

def main():
    # Refuse daytime launches before reading the catalog or contacting MCP.
    if not window_open():
        print(json.dumps({
            "state": "DEFERRED_OUTSIDE_RESEARCH_WINDOW",
            "new_york_time": datetime.now(NY).isoformat(),
            "allowed": "20:00 through 07:59 America/New_York",
            "broker_calls": 0,
            "orders_submitted": False,
        }, indent=2))
        return

    with (ROOT / "broker-link-review.lock").open("a") as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        asyncio.run(scan())

if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("Stopped. Saved checkpoints and completed archives retained.")
        raise SystemExit(130)
    except Exception as error:
        print("SCAN_STOPPED:", type(error).__name__, str(error))
        raise SystemExit(1)

========================================================================
FILE: /opt/si-equity-buy-worker/equity_buy_screen_v1.py
SHA256: df2e7b1e311cea1d7e3a9d3d78716dd641134f753ccd2a0368103e56f08fd9e7
"""BUY-only screening boundary. No activity on import.

Caller supplies read-only engine adapters and a current ticker-policy reader.
This module never submits orders or filters SELL monitoring.
"""
import copy
import inspect
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal

ENGINES = ("rules", "market_intelligence", "decision")


def now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def parsed(value):
    result = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    if result.tzinfo is None:
        raise ValueError("Timestamp timezone required")
    return result


def fresh(value, seconds):
    return 0 <= (now() - parsed(value)).total_seconds() <= seconds


def numeric(value):
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise ValueError("Boolean number rejected")
    result = Decimal(str(value))
    if not result.is_finite():
        raise ValueError("Nonfinite number rejected")
    return result


async def resolved(value):
    return await value if inspect.isawaitable(value) else value


async def screen_buy(snapshot, *, engines, buy_allowed):
    """
    Required snapshot:
      symbol, account, revision, parameters
      quote: bid, ask, bid_at, ask_at, observed_at
      positions_account, positions_checked_at, positions_complete
      positions: {symbol: {quantity, average_cost}}

    For an existing positive position:
      previous_quote: midpoint, observed_at

    Engine adapter response:
      symbol, account, revision, checked_at, passed, reasons

    Rules additionally returns checked_parameters covering every parameter.

    Adapters must validate their underlying evidence and configured rules.
    A fresh evaluation timestamp must not disguise stale source evidence.
    """
    symbol = snapshot.get("symbol") if isinstance(snapshot, dict) else None
    result = {
        "symbol": symbol,
        "state": "BLOCKED",
        "reasons": [],
        "engine_results": {},
        "order_authorized": False,
        "orders_submitted": False,
    }

    def reject(reason, excluded=False):
        result["state"] = "EXCLUDED" if excluded else "BLOCKED"
        result["reasons"].append(reason)
        return result

    async def buy_held():
        allowed = await resolved(buy_allowed(symbol))
        if type(allowed) is not bool:
            raise ValueError("Invalid BUY policy response")
        return not allowed

    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        return reject("INVALID_SNAPSHOT")

    s = copy.deepcopy(snapshot)

    # SI_EQUITY_NEWS_ADVISORY_V1
    # Informational only: do not alter engine verdicts or BUY/SELL gates.
    try:
        from equity_news_advisory_v1 import assess as _si_news_assess
        result["news_advisory"] = _si_news_assess(symbol)
    except Exception:
        result["news_advisory"] = {
            "status": "UNAVAILABLE",
            "reason": "ADVISORY_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE",
            "trade_risk_score": None,
            "execution_authorized": False,
        }


    if not isinstance(symbol, str) or not re.fullmatch(
        r"[A-Z][A-Z0-9.-]{0,9}", symbol
    ):
        return reject("INVALID_SYMBOL")

    try:
        if await buy_held():
            return reject("BUY_HELD", excluded=True)
    except Exception:
        return reject("BUY_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE")

    try:
        if not isinstance(s["account"], str) or not s["account"]:
            return reject("ACCOUNT_MISSING")

        if type(s["revision"]) is not int or s["revision"] < 1:
            return reject("INVALID_RULE_REVISION")

        if not isinstance(s["parameters"], dict) or not s["parameters"]:
            return reject("RULE_PARAMETERS_MISSING")

        if (
            s["positions_complete"] is not True
            or s["positions_account"] != s["account"]
            or not fresh(s["positions_checked_at"], 10)
            or not isinstance(s["positions"], dict)
        ):
            return reject("POSITIONS_NOT_VERIFIED")

        q = s["quote"]
        bid, ask = numeric(q["bid"]), numeric(q["ask"])

        if not 0 < bid <= ask:
            return reject("INVALID_QUOTE")

        quote_times = [
            q["bid_at"],
            q["ask_at"],
            q["observed_at"],
        ]

        if not all(fresh(t, 10) for t in quote_times):
            return reject("STALE_QUOTE")

        if symbol in s["positions"]:
            position = s["positions"][symbol]
            quantity = numeric(position["quantity"])

            if quantity < 0:
                return reject("SHORT_POSITION_REQUIRES_SEPARATE_POLICY")

            if quantity > 0:
                cost = numeric(position["average_cost"])
                previous = s["previous_quote"]
                previous_midpoint = numeric(previous["midpoint"])

                if cost <= 0 or previous_midpoint <= 0:
                    return reject("INVALID_POSITION_OR_TREND_DATA")

                if (
                    not fresh(previous["observed_at"], 25)
                    or parsed(previous["observed_at"])
                    >= parsed(q["observed_at"])
                ):
                    return reject("POSITION_TREND_NOT_VERIFIED")

                losing = bid < cost
                falling = (bid + ask) / 2 < previous_midpoint

                if losing and falling:
                    return reject(
                        "HELD_POSITION_LOSING_AND_FALLING",
                        excluded=True,
                    )

    except Exception:
        return reject("SCREENING_INPUTS_NOT_VERIFIED")

    if not isinstance(engines, dict):
        return reject("ENGINE_REGISTRY_NOT_VERIFIED")

    for name in ENGINES:
        adapter = engines.get(name)

        if not callable(adapter):
            result["reasons"].append("ENGINE_UNAVAILABLE:" + name)
            continue

        try:
            context = copy.deepcopy(s)
            context["engine_results"] = copy.deepcopy(
                result["engine_results"]
            )

            verdict = await resolved(adapter(context))

            if not isinstance(verdict, dict):
                raise ValueError("Invalid engine response")

            if (
                verdict.get("symbol") != symbol
                or verdict.get("account") != s["account"]
                or type(verdict.get("revision")) is not int
                or verdict["revision"] != s["revision"]
                or type(verdict.get("passed")) is not bool
                or not fresh(verdict.get("checked_at"), 10)
                or not isinstance(verdict.get("reasons"), list)
                or any(
                    not isinstance(reason, str)
                    for reason in verdict["reasons"]
                )
            ):
                raise ValueError("Unverified engine response")

            if name == "rules":
                checked = verdict.get("checked_parameters")
                if (
                    not isinstance(checked, list)
                    or any(not isinstance(key, str) for key in checked)
                    or not set(s["parameters"]).issubset(set(checked))
                ):
                    raise ValueError("Rule parameters not fully evaluated")

            result["engine_results"][name] = copy.deepcopy(verdict)

            if not verdict["passed"]:
                result["reasons"].append("ENGINE_REJECTED:" + name)

        except Exception:
            result["reasons"].append(
                "ENGINE_RESULT_NOT_VERIFIED:" + name
            )

    try:
        if await buy_held():
            return reject("BUY_HELD_AFTER_SCREENING", excluded=True)

        if (
            not all(fresh(t, 10) for t in quote_times)
            or not fresh(s["positions_checked_at"], 10)
            or any(
                not fresh(verdict["checked_at"], 10)
                for verdict in result["engine_results"].values()
            )
        ):
            return reject("SCREENING_EXPIRED_REFRESH_REQUIRED")

    except Exception:
        return reject("BUY_POLICY_OR_FRESHNESS_RECHECK_FAILED")

    if not result["reasons"]:
        result["state"] = "BUY_CANDIDATE"

    return result

FILE: /opt/si-equity-buy-worker/equity_commands_v1.py
SHA256: b07f4fe186fd7c514dffc6f382bdf7fcf55404674a3f00c0bce04e95472ad97c
"""Compatible Equity control queue. No writes on import."""
import argparse
import json
import os
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import UUID

DB=Path("/var/lib/si-equity-buy-worker/controls.sqlite3")
FIELDS={"bot","auto_trade","buy","sell"}
ROLE="sell_guard"

def now(): return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
def connect():
    db=sqlite3.connect(DB,timeout=10)
    db.row_factory=sqlite3.Row
    db.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=FULL")
    return db

def initialize():
    with connect() as db:
        db.executescript("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS equity_commands(
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,request_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
          requested_at TEXT NOT NULL,updates TEXT NOT NULL,state TEXT NOT NULL,
          completed_at TEXT,worker_pid INTEGER,result TEXT);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS equity_control_state(
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1),controls TEXT NOT NULL,
          acknowledged_at TEXT,worker_pid INTEGER);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS equity_worker_presence(
          role TEXT PRIMARY KEY,pid INTEGER NOT NULL,at TEXT NOT NULL);
        """)

def initialize_worker(role="sell_guard"):
    global ROLE
    if role not in {"sell_guard","buy_worker"}: raise ValueError("INVALID_WORKER_ROLE")
    ROLE=role
    initialize()
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO equity_control_state(id,controls) VALUES(1,?)",(json.dumps({"bot":True,"auto_trade":True,"buy":False,"sell":True}),))
        db.execute("INSERT INTO equity_worker_presence VALUES(?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(role) DO UPDATE SET pid=excluded.pid,at=excluded.at",(ROLE,os.getpid(),now()))

def validated_request(body):
    if not isinstance(body,dict) or set(body)!={"bot","updates","request_id"} or body["bot"]!="equity":
        raise ValueError("REQUIRE_EQUITY_COMMAND")
    UUID(body["request_id"])
    updates=body["updates"]
    if not isinstance(updates,dict) or not updates or not set(updates)<=FIELDS or any(type(v) is not bool for v in updates.values()):
        raise ValueError("REQUIRE_BOOLEAN_CONTROLS")
    return body["request_id"],json.dumps(updates,sort_keys=True)

def queue(body):
    ref,payload=validated_request(body)
    db=connect()
    try:
        db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        row=db.execute("SELECT * FROM equity_commands WHERE request_id=?",(ref,)).fetchone()
        if row:
            if row["updates"]!=payload: raise ValueError("REQUEST_ID_CONFLICT")
            id,state=row["id"],row["state"]
        else:
            if body["updates"].get("buy") is True:
                worker=db.execute("SELECT * FROM equity_worker_presence WHERE role='buy_worker'").fetchone()
                if not worker or not 0 <= (datetime.now(timezone.utc)-datetime.fromisoformat(worker["at"])).total_seconds() <= 210:
                    raise ValueError("BUY_WORKER_NOT_RECENTLY_OBSERVED")
            cur=db.execute("INSERT INTO equity_commands(request_id,requested_at,updates,state) VALUES(?,?,?,'QUEUED')",(ref,now(),payload))
            id,state=cur.lastrowid,"QUEUED"
        db.commit()
        return {"bot":"equity","request_id":ref,"command_id":id,"command_state":state,"worker_applied":state=="APPLIED","order_submitted_by_this_http_request":False}
    except BaseException:
        db.rollback()
        raise
    finally: db.close()

def apply_pending():
    db=connect()
    try:
        db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        row=db.execute("SELECT controls FROM equity_control_state WHERE id=1").fetchone()
        if not row: raise RuntimeError("EQUITY_CONTROL_STATE_MISSING")
        flags=json.loads(row["controls"])
        if set(flags)!=FIELDS or any(type(v) is not bool for v in flags.values()): raise RuntimeError("INVALID_EQUITY_CONTROL_STATE")
        for cmd in db.execute("SELECT * FROM equity_commands WHERE state='QUEUED' ORDER BY id").fetchall():
            try:
                _,payload=validated_request({"bot":"equity","request_id":cmd["request_id"],"updates":json.loads(cmd["updates"])})
                flags.update(json.loads(payload))
                state,result="APPLIED",{"controls":dict(flags),"applied_by_role":ROLE,"broker_execution_verified":False}
            except (ValueError,TypeError,KeyError):
                state,result="REJECTED",{"error":"INVALID_EQUITY_COMMAND"}
            db.execute("UPDATE equity_commands SET state=?,completed_at=?,worker_pid=?,result=? WHERE id=?",(state,now(),os.getpid(),json.dumps(result),cmd["id"]))
        db.execute("UPDATE equity_control_state SET controls=?,acknowledged_at=?,worker_pid=? WHERE id=1",(json.dumps(flags,sort_keys=True),now(),os.getpid()))
        db.execute("INSERT INTO equity_worker_presence VALUES(?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(role) DO UPDATE SET pid=excluded.pid,at=excluded.at",(ROLE,os.getpid(),now()))
        db.commit()
        return flags
    except BaseException:
        db.rollback()
        raise
    finally: db.close()

def require_sell_permission():
    c=apply_pending()
    if not all(c[k] for k in ("bot","auto_trade","sell")):
        raise RuntimeError("EQUITY_SELL_BLOCKED_BY_WORKER_CONTROLS")

def require_buy_permission():
    c=apply_pending()
    if not all(c[k] for k in ("bot","auto_trade","buy")):
        raise RuntimeError("EQUITY_BUY_BLOCKED_BY_WORKER_CONTROLS")

def snapshot():
    if not DB.is_file(): return {"bot":"equity","controls":None,"worker_report_fresh":False}
    with sqlite3.connect(DB.as_uri()+"?mode=ro",uri=True,timeout=5) as db:
        db.row_factory=sqlite3.Row
        db.execute("BEGIN")
        r=db.execute("SELECT * FROM equity_control_state WHERE id=1").fetchone()
        workers=[dict(x) for x in db.execute("SELECT * FROM equity_worker_presence")]
        commands=[dict(x) for x in db.execute("SELECT * FROM equity_commands ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 30")]
    elapsed=(datetime.now(timezone.utc)-datetime.fromisoformat(r["acknowledged_at"])).total_seconds() if r and r["acknowledged_at"] else None
    for c in commands:
        c["updates"]=json.loads(c["updates"])
        c["result"]=json.loads(c["result"]) if c["result"] else None
    return {"bot":"equity","controls":json.loads(r["controls"]) if r else None,"worker_report_fresh":elapsed is not None and 0<=elapsed<=210,"reported_worker_pid":r["worker_pid"] if r else None,"workers":workers,"commands":commands,"process_liveness_verified":False,"broker_execution_verified":False}

def history(): return snapshot().get("commands",[])

def main():
    import uuid
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("action",choices=["status","queue"])
    p.add_argument("--db",required=True)
    p.add_argument("--updates",help='JSON Boolean updates, e.g. {"buy":true}')
    args=p.parse_args()
    global DB
    DB=Path(args.db)
    result=snapshot() if args.action=="status" else queue({"bot":"equity","updates":json.loads(args.updates),"request_id":str(uuid.uuid4())})
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))

if __name__=="__main__": main()

FILE: /opt/si-equity-buy-worker/equity_news_advisory_v1.py
SHA256: a9caa770b5e13dc77228bd39afcf3b908f8adca49526d78bce1a56eb1750a613

"""Read-only news evidence. Never grants execution permission."""
import json
import os
import re
import stat
from datetime import datetime, timezone

REPORT = "/var/lib/si-global-news-v1/report.json"
SOURCE = "TipRanks via Google News"

def recent(value, now, limit):
    published = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    if published.tzinfo is None:
        return False
    return 0 <= (now - published).total_seconds() <= limit

def assess(symbol):
    result = {
        "status": "UNAVAILABLE",
        "source": SOURCE,
        "symbol": symbol,
        "evidence": [],
        "trade_risk_score": None,
        "execution_authorized": False,
        "affects_entry_permission": False,
        "affects_exit_permission": False,
    }
    fd = None
    try:
        if not isinstance(symbol, str) or not re.fullmatch(
            r"[A-Z][A-Z0-9.-]{0,9}", symbol
        ):
            result["reason"] = "INVALID_SYMBOL"
            return result

        fd = os.open(REPORT, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
        info = os.fstat(fd)
        if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_size > 3000000:
            raise ValueError("INVALID_REPORT_FILE")

        with os.fdopen(fd, encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            fd = None
            report = json.load(stream)

        if (
            report.get("version") != "SI_GLOBAL_NEWS_V1"
            or report.get("automatic_orders_enabled") is not False
        ):
            raise ValueError("INVALID_REPORT_CONTRACT")

        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if not recent(report["collected_at"], now, 600):
            result["reason"] = "REPORT_STALE_OR_FUTURE_DATED"
            return result

        state = report.get("sources", {}).get(SOURCE, {})
        if (
            state.get("status") != "READ_OK"
            or not recent(state["checked_at"], now, 600)
        ):
            result["reason"] = "SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE_OR_STALE"
            return result

        ticker = re.escape(symbol)
        mention = re.compile(
            r"(?:\$(?:" + ticker + r")(?![A-Z0-9.-])"
            r"|\b(?:NASDAQ|NYSE|AMEX)\s*:\s*" + ticker +
            r"(?![A-Z0-9.-])"
            r"|(?<![A-Z0-9.-])" + ticker +
            r"\s+(?i:stock|shares)\b)"
        )

        seen = set()
        for article in report.get("articles", []):
            if (
                article.get("source") != SOURCE
                or article.get("stale_source") is not False
                or article.get("publisher_attribution_checked") is not True
                or not recent(article["published_at"], now, 48 * 3600)
            ):
                continue

            title = article.get("title")
            if not isinstance(title, str) or not mention.search(title):
                continue

            identity = article.get("id")
            if not isinstance(identity, str) or identity in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(identity)

            result["evidence"].append({
                "id": identity,
                "title": title,
                "url": article.get("url"),
                "publisher": article.get("publisher"),
                "published_at": article["published_at"],
                "match_method": "EXPLICIT_TICKER_HEADLINE_MENTION",
                "instrument_identity_verified": False,
                "impact_verified": False,
            })

        result["evidence"] = result["evidence"][:10]
        result["status"] = (
            "REVIEW_REQUIRED" if result["evidence"]
            else "NO_EXPLICIT_TICKER_MATCH"
        )
        result["reason"] = (
            "Headline evidence only; no validated direction or trade risk. "
            "No match does not mean no risk."
        )
        return result

    except Exception as error:
        result["reason"] = "NEWS_EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE"
        result["error_type"] = type(error).__name__
        return result
    finally:
        if fd is not None:
            os.close(fd)

FILE: /opt/si-equity-buy-worker/equity_news_publish_v1.py
SHA256: a631d9fc98426894cfda1dfe4d0a7f2fd5e1f44de46b0de264e177db26d5c2f5

"""Publish headline evidence only. No broker calls or order permission."""
import json
import os
import tempfile
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from equity_news_advisory_v1 import assess

TARGET = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-buy-worker/news_advisory.json")
_last_attempt = None

def publish(symbols, held_symbols):
    global _last_attempt

    clock = time.monotonic()
    if _last_attempt is not None and clock - _last_attempt < 60:
        return
    _last_attempt = clock

    held = {
        symbol for symbol in held_symbols
        if isinstance(symbol, str)
    }
    universe = {
        symbol for symbol in symbols
        if isinstance(symbol, str)
    }

    # Existing holdings receive priority if the universe is very large.
    ordered = sorted(held) + sorted(universe - held)
    selected = ordered[:200]

    report = {
        "version": "SI_EQUITY_NEWS_ADVISORY_V1",
        "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "mode": "ADVISORY_ONLY",
        "execution_authorized": False,
        "affects_entry_permission": False,
        "affects_exit_permission": False,
        "symbols_requested": len(ordered),
        "symbols_assessed": len(selected),
        "symbols_omitted": len(ordered) - len(selected),
        "items": {},
    }

    for symbol in selected:
        item = assess(symbol)
        item["managed_position_present"] = symbol in held
        report["items"][symbol] = item

    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(
        prefix=".news-advisory-",
        dir=TARGET.parent,
    )
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            os.fchmod(stream.fileno(), 0o600)
            json.dump(report, stream, allow_nan=False)
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, TARGET)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary):
            os.unlink(temporary)

"""Standalone Equity worker. No live actions on import. See README before use."""

# SI_TICKER_HOLDS_V1
import sys as _si_holds_sys
