FILE: /opt/si-equity-buy-worker/research_session_v1.py
SHA256: 4b777fa2177a6934bb916c17de4538094c4a9ee3348a8017cec6053aa3124ef6
"""Off-hours research snapshots. No order execution or universe publishing."""
import ast
import fcntl
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
from collections import Counter
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

BASE = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-buy-worker")
RUNS = BASE / "research-runs"
CONFIG = Path("/etc/si-equity-buy-worker/config.json")
SCANNER = Path("/opt/si-equity-buy-worker/broker_link_offhours_v1.py")
NY = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")

def stamp():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def allowed():
    now = datetime.now(NY)
    return now.hour >= 20 or now.hour < 7 or (
        now.hour == 7 and now.minute < 59
    )

def digest(raw):
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()

def write_json(path, value):
    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".session-", dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as stream:
            os.fchmod(stream.fileno(), 0o600)
            json.dump(value, stream, separators=(",", ":"))
            stream.write("\n")
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary):
            os.unlink(temporary)

def write_bytes(path, raw):
    with path.open("xb") as stream:
        os.fchmod(stream.fileno(), 0o600)
        stream.write(raw)
        stream.flush()
        os.fsync(stream.fileno())

def prepare_review(catalog, source_hash):
    records = catalog.get("records")
    if (
        not isinstance(records, list)
        or not records
        or catalog.get("record_count") != len(records)
        or any(not isinstance(row, dict) for row in records)
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("INVALID_CATALOG")

    excluded = re.compile(
        r"\bwarrants?\b|\brights?\b|\bunits?\b|\bpreferred\b|"
        r"\bpreference\b|\bdepositary shares?\b|\bnotes?\b|"
        r"\bdebentures?\b|\bbonds?\b|\bbeneficial interest\b|"
        r"\bclosed[- ]end\b|\bETNs?\b|\bNextShares\b", re.I
    )
    common = re.compile(
        r"\bcommon stock\b|\bcommon shares?\b|"
        r"\bordinary shares?\b|\bAmerican Depositary Shares?\b|"
        r"\bAmerican Depositary Receipts?\b|\bADRs?\b", re.I
    )
    symbol_pattern = re.compile(r"[A-Z][A-Z0-9.-]{0,9}")
    duplicates = Counter(
        r.get("listing_symbol") for r in records
        if isinstance(r.get("listing_symbol"), str)
    )
    review, held = [], []
    counts = Counter()

    for row in records:
        symbol, name = row.get("listing_symbol"), row.get("security_name")
        reason, inferred = None, None
        if not isinstance(symbol, str) or not symbol_pattern.fullmatch(symbol):
            reason = "SYMBOL_REQUIRES_EXPLICIT_MAPPING"
        elif duplicates[symbol] != 1:
            reason = "DUPLICATE_LISTING_SYMBOL"
        elif not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip():
            reason = "SECURITY_DESCRIPTION_MISSING"
        elif excluded.search(name):
            reason = "OUT_OF_SCOPE_OR_COMPLEX_SECURITY_DESCRIPTION"
        elif row.get("financial_status") not in (None, "", "N"):
            reason = "NON_NORMAL_FINANCIAL_STATUS"
        elif row.get("is_etf") is True:
            inferred = "ETF_FLAGGED_BY_CATALOG"
        elif row.get("is_etf") is False and common.search(name):
            inferred = "COMMON_STOCK_OR_ADR_DESCRIPTION_MATCH"
        else:
            reason = "SECURITY_TYPE_UNRESOLVED"

        if reason:
            counts[reason] += 1
            held.append({
                "listing_symbol": symbol,
                "security_name": name,
                "reason": reason,
            })
        else:
            counts["REQUIRES_BROKER_AND_RISK_REVIEW"] += 1
            review.append({
                "listing_symbol": symbol,
                "security_name": name,
                "listing_exchange": row.get("listing_exchange"),
                "inferred_type": inferred,
                "classification_verified": False,
                "broker_symbol": None,
                "instrument_id": None,
                "broker_tradability_verified": False,
                "news_risk_verified": False,
                "earnings_risk_verified": False,
                "entry_authorized": False,
            })

    return {
        "state": "PRELIMINARY_SCREEN_ONLY_NOT_AUTHORIZED_UNIVERSE",
        "generated_at": stamp(),
        "source_fetched_at": catalog.get("fetched_at"),
        "source_sha256": source_hash,
        "source_freshness_verified": False,
        "source_record_count": len(records),
        "review_record_count": len(review),
        "held_record_count": len(held),
        "reason_counts": dict(counts),
        "classification_method": "CONSERVATIVE_DESCRIPTION_HEURISTICS",
        "classification_verified": False,
        "broker_tradability_verified": False,
        "news_risk_verified": False,
        "earnings_risk_verified": False,
        "entry_authorized": False,
        "records_for_review": review,
        "held_records": held,
    }

def validate_inputs(run):
    manifest = json.loads((run / "manifest.json").read_text())
    for name, expected in manifest["input_sha256"].items():
        if digest((run / name).read_bytes()) != expected:
            raise RuntimeError("FROZEN_INPUT_HASH_MISMATCH")
    return manifest

def main():
    if not allowed():
        print(json.dumps({
            "state": "DEFERRED_OUTSIDE_RESEARCH_WINDOW",
            "new_york_time": datetime.now(NY).isoformat(),
            "broker_calls": 0,
            "orders_submitted": False,
        }, indent=2))
        return

    cfg = json.loads(CONFIG.read_text())
    if (
        cfg.get("live_enabled") is not False
        or cfg.get("programmatic_execution_authorized") is not False
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("EXECUTION_FLAGS_MUST_REMAIN_FALSE")

    # Share the original lock with manually launched legacy scans.
    with (BASE / "broker-link-review.lock").open("a") as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)

        RUNS.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        now = datetime.now(NY)
        # A session runs from one evening through the following morning.
        session_date = (
            (now + timedelta(days=1)).date()
            if now.hour >= 20 else now.date()
        )
        run = RUNS / session_date.isoformat()

        if not run.exists():
            staging = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=".prepare-", dir=RUNS))
            try:
                raw = (BASE / "listing-catalog.json").read_bytes()
                catalog = json.loads(raw)
                review = prepare_review(catalog, digest(raw))

                write_bytes(staging / "listing-catalog.json", raw)
                write_json(staging / "listing-screen-review.json", review)

                hashes = {
                    "listing-catalog.json": digest(raw),
                    "listing-screen-review.json": digest(
                        (staging / "listing-screen-review.json").read_bytes()
                    ),
                }

                news = BASE / "google-news.json"
                if news.is_file():
                    news_raw = news.read_bytes()
                    json.loads(news_raw)
                    write_bytes(staging / "google-news.json", news_raw)
                    hashes["google-news.json"] = digest(news_raw)

                expected_identity = {
                    "source_sha256": digest(raw),
                    "review_sha256": digest(json.dumps(
                        review["records_for_review"], sort_keys=True
                    ).encode()),
                    "account_fingerprint": digest(
                        cfg["account_number"].encode()
                    ),
                }

                legacy = BASE / "broker-link-review.json"
                reused = False
                if legacy.is_file():
                    legacy_raw = legacy.read_bytes()
                    legacy_data = json.loads(legacy_raw)
                    if legacy_data.get("identity") == expected_identity:
                        write_bytes(
                            staging / "broker-link-review.json", legacy_raw
                        )
                        reused = True

                write_json(staging / "manifest.json", {
                    "session_id": session_date.isoformat(),
                    "created_at": stamp(),
                    "classification_rules_version": "description-heuristics-v1",
                    "input_sha256": hashes,
                    "legacy_checkpoint_reused": reused,
                    "entry_authorized": False,
                })
                os.rename(staging, run)
            finally:
                if staging.exists():
                    shutil.rmtree(staging)

        manifest = validate_inputs(run)
        if not allowed():
            print("DEFERRED: session prepared; research window closed.")
            return

        ast.parse(SCANNER.read_text(), filename=str(SCANNER))
        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
            "equity_frozen_session_scanner", str(SCANNER)
        )
        scanner = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        spec.loader.exec_module(scanner)

        # Redirect research files only; actual configuration stays unchanged.
        scanner.ROOT = run
        scanner.OUTPUT = run / "broker-link-review.json"
        scanner.ARCHIVE = run / "checkpoint-history"

        print(json.dumps({
            "session_id": manifest["session_id"],
            "research_directory": str(run),
            "legacy_checkpoint_reused": manifest["legacy_checkpoint_reused"],
            "entry_authorized": False,
        }, indent=2), flush=True)

        scanner.main()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except BlockingIOError:
        print("DEFERRED: another research scan holds the lock.")
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("Stopped. Research snapshots and saved batches retained.")
        raise SystemExit(130)
    except Exception as error:
        print("RESEARCH_STOPPED:", type(error).__name__, str(error))
        raise SystemExit(1)

FILE: /opt/si-equity-buy-worker/si100_entry_gate_v1.py
SHA256: e3d5f860f9d265332cf33451a8b0a75b8b6d903242aebfb7bb94732a36f94995
"""100-point BUY history validation. No broker calls or permission changes."""
from decimal import Decimal
import math
import time

class EntryHistoryBlocked(RuntimeError):
    pass

def evaluate(rows, now=None):
    now = time.time() if now is None else now
    if len(rows) != 60:
        raise EntryHistoryBlocked("ENTRY_REQUIRES_60_SAMPLES")
    timestamps = []
    prices = []
    for row in rows:
        if isinstance(row["t"], bool) or isinstance(row["price"], bool):
            raise EntryHistoryBlocked("ENTRY_INVALID_SAMPLE")
        t = float(row["t"])
        p = Decimal(str(row["price"]))
        if not math.isfinite(t) or not p.is_finite() or p <= 0:
            raise EntryHistoryBlocked("ENTRY_INVALID_SAMPLE")
        timestamps.append(t)
        prices.append(p)
    if not 0 <= now - timestamps[-1] <= 25:
        raise EntryHistoryBlocked("ENTRY_HISTORY_STALE_OR_FUTURE")
    if any(not 5 <= b-a <= 25 for a,b in zip(timestamps,timestamps[1:])):
        raise EntryHistoryBlocked("ENTRY_HISTORY_NOT_CONTINUOUS")
    factors = {
        "trend": sum(prices[-20:])/20 > sum(prices)/60,
        "momentum": prices[-1] > prices[-7],
        "breakout": prices[-1] > max(prices[-21:-1]),
    }
    score = 40*factors["trend"] + 30*factors["momentum"] + 30*factors["breakout"]
    if score != 100:
        raise EntryHistoryBlocked("ENTRY_SCORE_NOT_100:" + str(score))
    return {"score": score, "factors": factors}

def check_history(db, symbol=None):
    if symbol is None:
        rows = db.execute("SELECT t,price FROM samples ORDER BY t DESC LIMIT 60").fetchall()[::-1]
    else:
        rows = db.execute("SELECT t,price FROM samples WHERE symbol=? ORDER BY t DESC LIMIT 60", (symbol,)).fetchall()[::-1]
    return evaluate(rows)

========================================================================
FILE: /opt/si-equity-buy-worker/si_equity_sessions_v1.py
SHA256: 60c8461e000d31a855bea44cb63b883f55b1a5a68a15dd8c832c5ce16cc41e01
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pure session/order planning. No broker calls or execution approval."""
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_DOWN
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

NY = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


class SessionBlocked(RuntimeError):
    pass


def number(value):
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise SessionBlocked("BOOLEAN_NUMBER")
    try:
        result = Decimal(str(value))
    except Exception as error:
        raise SessionBlocked("INVALID_NUMBER") from error
    if not result.is_finite():
        raise SessionBlocked("NONFINITE_NUMBER")
    return result


def session_at(now=None):
    if now is None:
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if now.tzinfo is None or now.utcoffset() is None:
        raise SessionBlocked("TIMEZONE_REQUIRED")

    local = now.astimezone(NY)
    minute = local.hour * 60 + local.minute

    if local.weekday() >= 5 or not 360 <= minute < 1200:
        raise SessionBlocked("OUTSIDE_EQUITY_0600_2000")

    if 570 <= minute < 960:
        return "regular_hours"
    return "extended_hours"


def sized_quantity(raw_quantity, session):
    quantity = number(raw_quantity)
    if quantity <= 0:
        raise SessionBlocked("INVALID_QUANTITY")

    if session == "regular_hours":
        quantity = quantity.quantize(
            Decimal("0.000001"), rounding=ROUND_DOWN
        )
    elif session == "extended_hours":
        quantity = quantity.to_integral_value(rounding=ROUND_DOWN)
    else:
        raise SessionBlocked("UNKNOWN_SESSION")

    if quantity <= 0:
        raise SessionBlocked("INSUFFICIENT_CAPACITY_FOR_QUANTITY")
    return quantity


def require_tradability(row, account_category, session, quantity, side='buy'):
    if not isinstance(row, dict):
        raise SessionBlocked("INVALID_TRADABILITY")
    if row.get("tradeable") is not True or row.get("state") != "active":
        raise SessionBlocked("INSTRUMENT_NOT_TRADEABLE")
    if not isinstance(account_category, str) or not account_category:
        raise SessionBlocked("ACCOUNT_CATEGORY_REQUIRED")

    categories = row.get("account_type_tradabilities")
    if not isinstance(categories, list):
        raise SessionBlocked("ACCOUNT_TRADABILITY_UNKNOWN")
    matches = [
        item for item in categories
        if isinstance(item, dict)
        and item.get("account_type") == account_category
    ]
    if side not in {"buy", "sell"}:
        raise SessionBlocked("INVALID_SIDE")
    allowed_statuses = {"tradable"}
    if side == "sell":
        allowed_statuses.add("position_closing_only")
    if (
        len(matches) != 1
        or matches[0].get("account_type_tradability") not in allowed_statuses
    ):
        raise SessionBlocked("ACCOUNT_TRADABILITY_BLOCKED")

    halts = row.get("internal_halt_sessions")
    if halts is None:
        halts = []
    if not isinstance(halts, list):
        raise SessionBlocked("INVALID_HALT_SESSIONS")

    if session in halts:
        raise SessionBlocked("SESSION_HALTED")

    quantity = number(quantity)
    if quantity <= 0:
        raise SessionBlocked("INVALID_QUANTITY")

    if session == "extended_hours":
        if "all_day_hours" in halts:
            raise SessionBlocked("ALL_DAY_SESSION_HALTED")
        if row.get("all_day_tradability") != "tradable":
            raise SessionBlocked("EXTENDED_SESSION_NOT_ELIGIBLE")
        if quantity != quantity.to_integral_value():
            raise SessionBlocked("EXTENDED_FRACTIONAL_QUANTITY")
    elif session == "regular_hours":
        if (
            quantity != quantity.to_integral_value()
            and row.get("fractional_tradability") != "tradable"
        ):
            raise SessionBlocked("FRACTIONAL_NOT_ELIGIBLE")
    else:
        raise SessionBlocked("UNKNOWN_SESSION")


def order_fields(side, quantity, bid, ask, session):
    if side not in {"buy", "sell"}:
        raise SessionBlocked("INVALID_SIDE")

    quantity, bid, ask = map(number, (quantity, bid, ask))
    if quantity <= 0 or not 0 < bid <= ask:
        raise SessionBlocked("INVALID_ORDER_VALUES")
    if quantity != quantity.quantize(Decimal("0.000001")):
        raise SessionBlocked("INVALID_QUANTITY_PRECISION")

    fields = {
        "side": side,
        "quantity": str(quantity),
        "time_in_force": "gfd",
        "market_hours": session,
    }

    if session == "regular_hours":
        fields["type"] = "market"
    elif session == "extended_hours":
        if quantity != quantity.to_integral_value():
            raise SessionBlocked("EXTENDED_FRACTIONAL_EXIT_OR_ORDER")
        fields["type"] = "limit"
        fields["limit_price"] = str(ask if side == "buy" else bid)
    else:
        raise SessionBlocked("UNKNOWN_SESSION")

    return fields


def require_same_session(expected, now=None):
    if session_at(now) != expected:
        raise SessionBlocked("SESSION_CHANGED_REVIEW_REQUIRED")

FILE: /opt/si-equity-r1/broker_monitor.py
SHA256: fc071d3e0e2f998b446f7125e8fe23b0ffa2c67446508a5af14bc50ec977fc08
import argparse
import asyncio
import inspect
import json
import os
import sqlite3
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, "/home/botuser")
import si_mcp_auth as auth

ROOT = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-r1")
ALLOWED = {
    "get_accounts", "get_portfolio",
    "get_equity_positions", "get_equity_orders",
}
TERMINAL = {
    "filled", "cancelled", "rejected", "failed",
    "voided", "partially_filled_rest_cancelled",
}

def now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def database():
    db = sqlite3.connect(ROOT / "monitor.sqlite3", timeout=10)
    db.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=FULL")
    db.executescript("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snapshots(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            at TEXT NOT NULL,
            summary TEXT NOT NULL,
            broker_data TEXT NOT NULL
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS health(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1),
            at TEXT NOT NULL,
            payload TEXT NOT NULL
        );
    """)
    db.commit()
    return db

def save_health(db, payload):
    db.execute(
        "INSERT INTO health VALUES(1,?,?) "
        "ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET at=excluded.at,payload=excluded.payload",
        (now(), json.dumps(payload, allow_nan=False)),
    )
    db.commit()

async def refuse_login(*args, **kwargs):
    raise RuntimeError("INTERACTIVE_AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED")

async def read_tool(session, name, arguments):
    if name not in ALLOWED:
        raise RuntimeError("NON_READ_ONLY_TOOL_REFUSED")
    params = inspect.signature(session.call_tool).parameters
    if "arguments" in params:
        result = await session.call_tool(name, arguments=arguments)
    elif "params" in params:
        from mcp.types import CallToolRequestParams
        result = await session.call_tool(
            params=CallToolRequestParams(name=name, arguments=arguments)
        )
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("UNSUPPORTED_MCP_CALL_SIGNATURE")

    envelope = result.model_dump(mode="json", by_alias=True)
    if envelope.get("isError") or envelope.get("is_error"):
        raise RuntimeError("BROKER_TOOL_ERROR")

    payload = envelope.get(
        "structuredContent", envelope.get("structured_content")
    )
    if payload is None:
        texts = [
            item["text"] for item in envelope.get("content", [])
            if item.get("type") == "text" and "text" in item
        ]
        payload = json.loads("\n".join(texts))

    if not isinstance(payload, dict) or not isinstance(payload.get("data"), dict):
        raise RuntimeError("UNEXPECTED_BROKER_RESPONSE")
    return payload["data"]

async def pages(session, name, account, field):
    collected = []
    seen = set()
    cursor = None
    for _ in range(100):
        args = {"account_number": account}
        if cursor:
            args["cursor"] = cursor
        data = await read_tool(session, name, args)
        rows = data.get(field)
        if rows is not None and not isinstance(rows, list):
            raise RuntimeError("INVALID_PAGINATED_RESPONSE")
        for row in rows or []:
            if row is not None:
                if not isinstance(row, dict):
                    raise RuntimeError("INVALID_BROKER_ROW")
                collected.append(row)
        cursor = data.get("next")
        if not cursor:
            return collected
        if not isinstance(cursor, str) or cursor in seen:
            raise RuntimeError("INVALID_OR_REPEATED_CURSOR")
        seen.add(cursor)
    raise RuntimeError("PAGINATION_LIMIT")

async def snapshot(config):
    oauth = auth.OAuthClientProvider(
        server_url=auth.SERVER,
        client_metadata=auth.OAuthClientMetadata(
            client_name="super-intelligence-vps",
            redirect_uris=[auth.REDIRECT],
            grant_types=["authorization_code", "refresh_token"],
            response_types=["code"],
            token_endpoint_auth_method="none",
        ),
        storage=auth.FileStorage(),
        redirect_handler=refuse_login,
        callback_handler=refuse_login,
    )
    async with auth.httpx2.AsyncClient(
        auth=oauth,
        timeout=auth.httpx2.Timeout(30.0, read=120.0),
    ) as client:
        async with auth.streamable_http_client(
            auth.SERVER, http_client=client
        ) as streams:
            async with auth.ClientSession(streams[0], streams[1]) as session:
                await session.initialize()
                data = await read_tool(session, "get_accounts", {})
                accounts = data.get("accounts")
                if accounts is not None and not isinstance(accounts, list):
                    raise RuntimeError("INVALID_ACCOUNTS_RESPONSE")
                matches = [
                    item for item in accounts or []
                    if isinstance(item, dict)
                    and item.get("agentic_allowed") is True
                    and str(item.get("account_number", "")).endswith(
                        config["account_last4"]
                    )
                ]
                if len(matches) != 1:
                    raise RuntimeError("ACCOUNT_BINDING_NOT_VERIFIED")
                account = matches[0]
                if (
                    account.get("state") != "active"
                    or account.get("deactivated") is not False
                    or account.get("permanently_deactivated") is not False
                ):
                    raise RuntimeError("ACCOUNT_NOT_ACTIVE")

                number = account["account_number"]
                portfolio = await read_tool(
                    session, "get_portfolio", {"account_number": number}
                )
                positions = await pages(
                    session, "get_equity_positions", number, "positions"
                )
                orders = await pages(
                    session, "get_equity_orders", number, "orders"
                )
                return portfolio, positions, orders

async def run():
    config = json.loads((ROOT / "config.json").read_text())
    if (
        config.get("mode") != "READ_ONLY_BROKER_MONITOR"
        or config.get("entries_enabled") is not False
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("READ_ONLY_CONFIGURATION_REQUIRED")

    db = database()
    try:
        while True:
            save_health(db, {
                "state": "COLLECTING",
                "mode": "READ_ONLY",
                "entries_enabled": False,
                "exit_protection_implemented": False,
            })
            try:
                portfolio, positions, orders = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    snapshot(config), timeout=180
                )
                buying_power = portfolio.get("buying_power")
                summary = {
                    "state": "BROKER_READ_VERIFIED",
                    "checked_at": now(),
                    "mode": "READ_ONLY",
                    "account": "****" + config["account_last4"],
                    "currency": portfolio.get("currency"),
                    "buying_power": buying_power,
                    "capital_snapshot": config["capital_snapshot"],
                    "capital_snapshot_is_live_authorization": False,
                    "broker_position_rows": len(positions),
                    "broker_order_rows": len(orders),
                    "nonterminal_order_rows": sum(
                        item.get("state") not in TERMINAL for item in orders
                    ),
                    "entries_enabled": False,
                    "positions_adopted": False,
                    "order_submission_implemented": False,
                    "exit_protection_implemented": False,
                    "master_strategy_implemented": False,
                }
                db.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO snapshots(at,summary,broker_data) VALUES(?,?,?)",
                    (
                        now(), json.dumps(summary, allow_nan=False),
                        json.dumps({
                            "portfolio": portfolio,
                            "positions": positions,
                            "orders": orders,
                        }, allow_nan=False),
                    ),
                )
                db.execute(
                    "DELETE FROM snapshots WHERE id NOT IN "
                    "(SELECT id FROM snapshots ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 100)"
                )
                db.commit()
                save_health(db, summary)
                print(json.dumps(summary, allow_nan=False), flush=True)
            except Exception as error:
                failure = {
                    "state": "BROKER_READ_FAILED",
                    "checked_at": now(),
                    "error_type": type(error).__name__,
                    "entries_enabled": False,
                    "order_submission_implemented": False,
                    "exit_protection_implemented": False,
                }
                save_health(db, failure)
                print(json.dumps(failure), flush=True)
            await asyncio.sleep(30)
    finally:
        db.close()

def status():
    path = ROOT / "monitor.sqlite3"
    if not path.is_file():
        print(json.dumps({"state": "NO_MONITOR_DATABASE"}))
        return
    with sqlite3.connect("file:" + str(path) + "?mode=ro", uri=True) as db:
        row = db.execute("SELECT at,payload FROM health WHERE id=1").fetchone()
        latest = db.execute(
            "SELECT at,summary FROM snapshots ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
        ).fetchone()
    print(json.dumps({
        "current_health": (
            {"at": row[0], "payload": json.loads(row[1])} if row else None
        ),
        "last_successful_snapshot": (
            {"at": latest[0], "payload": json.loads(latest[1])}
            if latest else None
        ),
    }, indent=2))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    os.umask(0o077)
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("action", choices=["run", "status"])
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.action == "run":
        asyncio.run(run())
    else:
        status()

FILE: /opt/si-equity-r1/earnings_collector_v1.py
SHA256: 49d89babe477873b16e65692fed298c87981d8b49791bd4861dd5d00064f45a9
import asyncio
import json
import os
import sys
import tempfile
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, "/opt/si-equity-r1")
import broker_monitor as monitor

auth = monitor.auth
TOOL = "get_earnings_results"
SYMBOLS = ("SPCX", "IONQ")
DESTINATION = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-r1/earnings_latest.json")
monitor.ALLOWED.add(TOOL)

def arguments_for(schema, symbol):
    if not isinstance(schema, dict):
        raise RuntimeError("INPUT_SCHEMA_MISSING")
    properties = schema.get("properties", {})
    required = schema.get("required", [])

    symbols = properties.get("symbols", {})
    symbol_field = properties.get("symbol", {})
    kinds = symbols.get("type", [])
    if isinstance(kinds, str):
        kinds = [kinds]

    if (
        "array" in kinds
        and symbols.get("items", {}).get("type") == "string"
    ):
        arguments = {"symbols": [symbol]}
    elif symbol_field.get("type") == "string":
        arguments = {"symbol": symbol}
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("TICKER_ARGUMENT_REQUIRES_EXPLICIT_ADAPTER")

    unresolved = [name for name in required if name not in arguments]
    if unresolved:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "ADDITIONAL_REQUIRED_ARGUMENTS: " + ", ".join(unresolved)
        )
    return arguments

def save(report):
    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(
        dir=DESTINATION.parent, prefix=".earnings-"
    )
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as output:
            json.dump(report, output, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
            output.write("\n")
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        os.chmod(temporary, 0o600)
        os.replace(temporary, DESTINATION)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary):
            os.unlink(temporary)

async def main():
    oauth = auth.OAuthClientProvider(
        server_url=auth.SERVER,
        client_metadata=auth.OAuthClientMetadata(
            client_name="super-intelligence-vps",
            redirect_uris=[auth.REDIRECT],
            grant_types=["authorization_code", "refresh_token"],
            response_types=["code"],
            token_endpoint_auth_method="none",
        ),
        storage=auth.FileStorage(),
        redirect_handler=monitor.refuse_login,
        callback_handler=monitor.refuse_login,
    )

    report = {
        "collected_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "mode": "READ_ONLY_EARNINGS",
        "tool": TOOL,
        "orders_submitted": False,
        "entry_authorized": False,
        "earnings_contributions": {symbol: None for symbol in SYMBOLS},
        "responses": {},
    }

    async with auth.httpx2.AsyncClient(
        auth=oauth,
        timeout=auth.httpx2.Timeout(30.0, read=120.0),
    ) as client:
        async with auth.streamable_http_client(
            auth.SERVER, http_client=client
        ) as streams:
            async with auth.ClientSession(
                streams[0], streams[1]
            ) as session:
                await session.initialize()
                tools = []
                cursor = None
                seen = set()

                for _ in range(100):
                    page = (
                        await session.list_tools()
                        if cursor is None
                        else await session.list_tools(cursor=cursor)
                    )
                    tools.extend(page.tools)
                    cursor = getattr(page, "nextCursor", None)
                    if not cursor:
                        break
                    if cursor in seen:
                        raise RuntimeError("REPEATED_TOOL_CURSOR")
                    seen.add(cursor)
                else:
                    raise RuntimeError("TOOL_PAGINATION_LIMIT")

                matches = [tool for tool in tools if tool.name == TOOL]
                if len(matches) != 1:
                    raise RuntimeError("EARNINGS_TOOL_NOT_UNIQUE")

                definition = matches[0].model_dump(
                    mode="json", by_alias=True
                )
                contract = {
                    key: definition.get(key)
                    for key in (
                        "name", "description", "inputSchema", "outputSchema"
                    )
                }
                report["contract"] = contract

                print("=== TICKER-SPECIFIC EARNINGS CONTRACT ===")
                print(json.dumps(contract, indent=2, allow_nan=False))

                requests = {
                    symbol: arguments_for(contract["inputSchema"], symbol)
                    for symbol in SYMBOLS
                }
                for symbol in SYMBOLS:
                    data = await monitor.read_tool(
                        session, TOOL, requests[symbol]
                    )
                    result = {
                        "arguments": requests[symbol],
                        "received_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                        "data": data,
                    }
                    report["responses"][symbol] = result
                    print("\n=== EARNINGS RESPONSE:", symbol, "===")
                    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, allow_nan=False))

    report["state"] = "EARNINGS_DATA_COLLECTED_NOT_YET_SCORED"
    save(report)
    print("\nSaved:", DESTINATION)
    print("No orders submitted. No services or capital settings changed.")
    print("Earnings contributions remain unknown until the data is interpreted.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    os.umask(0o077)
    try:
        asyncio.run(main())
    except Exception as error:
        print("COLLECTION STOPPED:", type(error).__name__)
        if isinstance(error, RuntimeError):
            print(str(error))
        print("This attempt did not replace the saved report.")
        print("No order authorization was granted.")
        sys.exit(1)

========================================================================
FILE: /opt/si-equity-r1/entry_coordinator_v2.py
SHA256: d3a6d210de9778ceb109791e015f0ebabbacc1ead4cb9788755cbc2501893358
"""Equity entry coordinator; no activity at import.

Required adapter contract:
- snapshot(order): returns verified inputs for entry_policy_v2.evaluate().
- review(order): raises unless broker review passes and unattended execution
  is permitted. It must not submit an order.
- submit(order, ref_id): submits exactly once and returns the broker order ID.
- budget(order): returns verified capital available before this ledger's
  reservations, excluding deployed capital and other commitments.

All adapter callbacks are async.
The order quantity is a share quantity, not a dollar amount.
maximum_cost must conservatively include price limits and fees.
This coordinator does not support unbounded market-order cost estimates.

No automatic recovery, cancellation, reservation release, or fill adoption.
Do not connect live entries until exit coverage and reconciliation exist.
"""
import fcntl
from pathlib import Path

import entry_policy_v2 as policy
import entry_ledger_v2 as ledger

LOCK = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-stop-guard/entry-coordinator-v2.lock")


def blocked(reason, **details):
    return {
        "state": "BLOCKED",
        "reason": reason,
        "orders_submitted": False,
        **details,
    }


def verify_binding(snapshot, order):
    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        raise RuntimeError("INVALID_ADAPTER_SNAPSHOT")
    if (
        snapshot.get("symbol") != order["symbol"]
        or snapshot.get("account") != order["account"]
        or snapshot.get("desired_revision") != order["revision"]
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("SNAPSHOT_ORDER_BINDING_FAILED")
    if (
        policy.number(snapshot.get("maximum_order_cost_with_fees"))
        != policy.number(order["maximum_cost"])
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("SNAPSHOT_COST_BINDING_FAILED")


async def attempt(
    request_id, order, *, snapshot, review, submit, budget
):
    """Database must be explicitly initialized during a future deployment.

    A RESERVED row from a prior attempt is not automatically reused.
    This is deliberately conservative until recovery is implemented.
    """
    # SI_ENTRY_COORDINATOR_DISABLED_PENDING_INTEGRATION
    return blocked(
        "EXECUTION_DISABLED_PENDING_COMPLETE_INTEGRATION"
    )

    with LOCK.open("a") as lock:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:
            return blocked("ANOTHER_ENTRY_ATTEMPT_IS_RUNNING")

        # Take a copy so callbacks cannot alter the order being authorized.
        original = dict(order)

        first = await snapshot(dict(original))
        verify_binding(first, original)
        decision = policy.evaluate(first)
        if not decision["entry_authorized"]:
            return blocked(
                "POLICY_REJECTED", blockers=decision["blockers"]
            )

        await review(dict(original))

        # Refresh risk, permissions, and capital after broker review.
        current = await snapshot(dict(original))
        verify_binding(current, original)
        decision = policy.evaluate(current)
        if not decision["entry_authorized"]:
            return blocked(
                "POST_REVIEW_POLICY_REJECTED",
                blockers=decision["blockers"],
            )

        available = await budget(dict(original))
        reservation = ledger.reserve(
            request_id, dict(original), available
        )
        if not reservation["new_reservation"]:
            return blocked(
                "EXISTING_INTENT_REQUIRES_RECONCILIATION",
                request_id=reservation["request_id"],
                intent_state=reservation["state"],
            )

        # If this check fails, retain the reservation for reconciliation.
        final = await snapshot(dict(original))
        verify_binding(final, original)
        decision = policy.evaluate(final)
        if not decision["entry_authorized"]:
            return blocked(
                "RESERVED_BUT_FINAL_POLICY_REJECTED",
                reservation_retained=True,
                blockers=decision["blockers"],
            )

        reference = reservation["request_id"]
        ledger.mark_submitting(reference)

        try:
            broker_id = await submit(dict(original), reference)
            ledger.record_broker_id(reference, broker_id)
        except BaseException:
            # Includes cancellation. Submission may have reached the broker.
            # Leave SUBMITTING in the database; never automatically resend.
            raise RuntimeError(
                "SUBMISSION_OUTCOME_UNRESOLVED_DO_NOT_RESUBMIT:"
                + reference
            ) from None

        return {
            "state": "BROKER_ACKNOWLEDGED_NOT_FILL_VERIFIED",
            "request_id": reference,
            "broker_id": broker_id,
            "submission_attempted": True,
            "fill_verified": False,
            "reservation_retained": True,
        }

========================================================================
FILE: /opt/si-equity-r1/entry_ledger_v2.py
SHA256: d2ebe972d9227eeac4d7b22ef302f927d5d863566ee155cb9e82b58413c58051
"""Staged Equity entry ledger. Importing performs no I/O.

No broker calls. No automatic release of reservations.
The adapter must serialize budget calculation, reservation, and submission.
A missing submission response is not permission to submit again.
"""
import json
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import UUID

DB = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-stop-guard/entry-ledger-v2.sqlite3")


def now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def amount(value):
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise ValueError("INVALID_AMOUNT")
    try:
        result = Decimal(str(value))
    except (InvalidOperation, ValueError):
        raise ValueError("INVALID_AMOUNT") from None
    if not result.is_finite() or result < 0:
        raise ValueError("INVALID_AMOUNT")
    return result


def connect():
    db = sqlite3.connect(DB, timeout=10)
    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    db.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=FULL")
    return db


def initialize():
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS entry_intents(
                request_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                payload TEXT NOT NULL,
                reserved_cost TEXT NOT NULL,
                state TEXT NOT NULL,
                broker_id TEXT UNIQUE,
                created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
            )
        """)


def reserve(request_id, order, budget_before_ledger_reservations):
    """Budget must already exclude deployed capital and other commitments.

    This function subtracts this ledger's reservations exactly once.
    It does not verify the supplied budget against the broker or allocation.
    """
    request_id = str(UUID(request_id))
    if not isinstance(order, dict):
        raise ValueError("INVALID_ORDER")
    if set(order) != {
        "account", "symbol", "quantity", "maximum_cost", "revision"
    }:
        raise ValueError("INVALID_ORDER_FIELDS")
    if any(
        not isinstance(order[key], str) or not order[key]
        for key in ("account", "symbol")
    ):
        raise ValueError("MISSING_ORDER_IDENTITY")
    if amount(order["quantity"]) <= 0:
        raise ValueError("INVALID_QUANTITY")
    cost = amount(order["maximum_cost"])
    if cost <= 0:
        raise ValueError("INVALID_COST")
    if type(order["revision"]) is not int or order["revision"] < 1:
        raise ValueError("INVALID_REVISION")

    budget = amount(budget_before_ledger_reservations)
    payload = json.dumps(
        order, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False
    )
    db = connect()
    try:
        db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        saved = db.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM entry_intents WHERE request_id=?",
            (request_id,),
        ).fetchone()
        if saved:
            if saved["payload"] != payload:
                raise ValueError("REQUEST_ID_CONTENT_CONFLICT")
            db.commit()
            return {**dict(saved), "new_reservation": False}

        rows = db.execute(
            "SELECT payload,reserved_cost FROM entry_intents"
        ).fetchall()
        committed = sum(
            (
                amount(row["reserved_cost"])
                for row in rows
                if json.loads(row["payload"])["account"] == order["account"]
            ),
            Decimal(0),
        )
        if committed + cost > budget:
            raise RuntimeError("INSUFFICIENT_UNRESERVED_BUDGET")

        stamp = now()
        db.execute(
            "INSERT INTO entry_intents VALUES(?,?,?,'RESERVED',NULL,?,?)",
            (request_id, payload, str(cost), stamp, stamp),
        )
        db.commit()
        return {
            "request_id": request_id,
            "state": "RESERVED",
            "reserved_cost": str(cost),
            "new_reservation": True,
        }
    except Exception:
        db.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        db.close()


def mark_submitting(request_id):
    """Persist BEFORE the broker call. A second attempt is refused."""
    request_id = str(UUID(request_id))
    with connect() as db:
        cursor = db.execute(
            "UPDATE entry_intents SET state='SUBMITTING',updated_at=? "
            "WHERE request_id=? AND state='RESERVED'",
            (now(), request_id),
        )
        if cursor.rowcount != 1:
            raise RuntimeError("SUBMISSION_ALREADY_STARTED_OR_NOT_RESERVED")


def record_broker_id(request_id, broker_id):
    """An acknowledgement is not a verified fill."""
    request_id = str(UUID(request_id))
    if not isinstance(broker_id, str) or not broker_id:
        raise ValueError("BROKER_ID_REQUIRED")
    with connect() as db:
        row = db.execute(
            "SELECT state,broker_id FROM entry_intents WHERE request_id=?",
            (request_id,),
        ).fetchone()
        if row is None:
            raise RuntimeError("INTENT_NOT_FOUND")
        if row["state"] == "ACKNOWLEDGED" and row["broker_id"] == broker_id:
            return
        if row["state"] != "SUBMITTING" or row["broker_id"] is not None:
            raise RuntimeError("BROKER_ACK_CONFLICT")
        db.execute(
            "UPDATE entry_intents SET state='ACKNOWLEDGED',"
            "broker_id=?,updated_at=? WHERE request_id=?",
            (broker_id, now(), request_id),
        )

========================================================================
FILE: /opt/si-equity-r1/entry_policy_v2.py
SHA256: 25a22a86dc6e0d4ff2bb48a4aa9ed440521ae8eaa2d0587b285ea877b1eb44ae
"""Fail-closed Equity entry-policy boundary.

No broker calls, database writes, permission changes, or automatic execution.
Authorization is valid only for the supplied snapshot. A submission adapter
must reserve capital atomically and revalidate immediately before submission.
"""
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation

VERSION = "equity-entry-policy-v2"

REQUIRED_CHECKS = (
    "approved_entry_signal",
    "news_risk",
    "earnings_risk",
    "instrument_eligibility",
    "market_regime",
    "buy_restrictions",
    "daily_loss",
    "exit_coverage",
)


def number(value):
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(
        value, (str, int, float, Decimal)
    ):
        raise ValueError("INVALID_NUMBER")
    try:
        result = Decimal(str(value))
    except InvalidOperation:
        raise ValueError("INVALID_NUMBER") from None
    if not result.is_finite():
        raise ValueError("NONFINITE_NUMBER")
    return result


def fresh(stamp, now, max_age):
    if not isinstance(stamp, str):
        return False
    try:
        parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(stamp.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        if parsed.tzinfo is None:
            return False
        age = (now - parsed).total_seconds()
        return 0 <= age <= max_age
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
        return False


def evaluate(snapshot):
    blockers = []
    result = {
        "policy_version": VERSION,
        "entry_authorized": False,
        "orders_submitted": False,
        "capital_reserved": False,
        "blockers": blockers,
    }

    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        blockers.append("INVALID_SNAPSHOT")
        return result

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    symbol = snapshot.get("symbol")
    result["symbol"] = symbol

    if (
        not isinstance(symbol, str)
        or not symbol
        or len(symbol) > 10
        or not symbol[0].isalpha()
        or not symbol.isascii()
        or symbol != symbol.upper()
        or any(not (c.isalnum() or c in ".-") for c in symbol)
    ):
        blockers.append("INVALID_SYMBOL")

    if not fresh(snapshot.get("checked_at"), now, 10):
        blockers.append("STALE_OR_INVALID_SNAPSHOT")

    controls = snapshot.get("controls")
    if not isinstance(controls, dict) or any(
        controls.get(key) is not True
        for key in ("bot", "auto_trade", "buy", "sell")
    ):
        blockers.append("REQUIRED_PERMISSIONS_NOT_ENABLED")

    if snapshot.get("account_verified") is not True:
        blockers.append("ACCOUNT_NOT_VERIFIED")

    if snapshot.get("unresolved_order") is not False:
        blockers.append("ORDER_OUTCOME_NOT_CLEAR")

    revision = snapshot.get("desired_revision")
    if (
        type(revision) is not int
        or revision < 1
        or type(snapshot.get("applied_revision")) is not int
        or snapshot["applied_revision"] != revision
    ):
        blockers.append("SETTINGS_NOT_APPLIED")

    checks = snapshot.get("checks")
    if not isinstance(checks, dict):
        checks = {}

    for name in REQUIRED_CHECKS:
        check = checks.get(name)
        if (
            not isinstance(check, dict)
            or check.get("passed") is not True
            or check.get("symbol") != symbol
            or check.get("revision") != revision
            or not fresh(check.get("checked_at"), now, 10)
        ):
            blockers.append("GATE_NOT_VERIFIED:" + name)

    try:
        count = snapshot["open_and_reserved_positions"]
        limit = snapshot["max_positions"]
        if (
            type(count) is not int
            or type(limit) is not int
            or count < 0
            or not 1 <= limit <= 100
            or count >= limit
        ):
            blockers.append("POSITION_LIMIT_NOT_VERIFIED")
    except KeyError:
        blockers.append("POSITION_LIMIT_MISSING")

    try:
        if snapshot.get("order_type") == "market":
            if (
                snapshot.get("market_hours") != "regular_hours"
                or snapshot.get("cost_is_guaranteed") is not False
            ):
                blockers.append("INVALID_MARKET_ESTIMATE_CONTRACT")
            required = number(snapshot["estimated_order_cost_with_fees"])
            result["cost_basis"] = "ESTIMATED_MARKET_COST"
            result["price_cap_guaranteed"] = False
        else:
            required = number(snapshot["maximum_order_cost_with_fees"])
        bot_available = number(snapshot["bot_available_after_reservations"])
        broker_available = number(
            snapshot["broker_available_after_reservations"]
        )
        per_order_limit = number(snapshot["approved_per_order_limit"])
        if (
            required <= 0
            or min(bot_available, broker_available, per_order_limit) < 0
            or required > min(
                bot_available, broker_available, per_order_limit
            )
        ):
            blockers.append("CAPITAL_OR_ORDER_LIMIT_FAILED")
    except (KeyError, ValueError):
        blockers.append("CAPITAL_NOT_VERIFIED")

    result["entry_authorized"] = not blockers
    result["next_step"] = (
        "ATOMIC_RESERVATION_AND_PRETRADE_REVALIDATION"
        if not blockers else "BLOCKED"
    )
    return result

========================================================================
FILE: /opt/si-equity-r1/entry_signals_v1.py
SHA256: daee0ce1057d4a17ee793904c32da0e23a09544745aaeebcb98fdd272d8d0948
import argparse
import json
import math
import re
import sys
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from urllib.parse import urlencode, quote
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

VERSION = "equity-technical-proposal-v1"
NY = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")

# New proposed technical score, maximum 10:
# MA9 > MA21:             +2
# MA21 > MA50:            +2
# Close > MA200:          +1
# Close > session VWAP:   +1
# MACD histogram > 0:     +1
# RSI14 between 30–65:    +1
# Volume ratio >= 1.5:    +1
# Five-bar momentum > 0:  +1
#
# This is NOT an order-authorization formula.
# No missing intelligence score is replaced with zero.

class DataRejected(RuntimeError):
    pass

def numeric(value, name):
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        raise DataRejected("INVALID_" + name)
    result = float(value)
    if not math.isfinite(result):
        raise DataRejected("NONFINITE_" + name)
    return result

def ema(values, period):
    alpha = 2 / (period + 1)
    result = [values[0]]
    for value in values[1:]:
        result.append(alpha * value + (1-alpha) * result[-1])
    return result

def rsi(values, period=14):
    differences = [b-a for a, b in zip(values, values[1:])]
    gain = sum(max(value, 0) for value in differences[:period]) / period
    loss = sum(max(-value, 0) for value in differences[:period]) / period
    for value in differences[period:]:
        gain = (gain*(period-1) + max(value, 0)) / period
        loss = (loss*(period-1) + max(-value, 0)) / period
    if loss == 0:
        return 100 if gain > 0 else 50
    return 100 - 100/(1 + gain/loss)

def wilder_average(values, period=14):
    result = sum(values[:period]) / period
    for value in values[period:]:
        result = (result*(period-1) + value) / period
    return result

def download(symbol):
    end = int(time.time())
    params = urlencode({
        "period1": end - 10*24*60*60,
        "period2": end,
        "interval": "5m",
        "includePrePost": "false",
    })
    url = (
        "https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/"
        + quote(symbol, safe="") + "?" + params
    )
    request = Request(url, headers={
        "User-Agent": "SI-Equity-Technical-Scanner/1.0",
        "Accept": "application/json",
    })
    with urlopen(request, timeout=20) as response:
        payload = json.load(response)

    chart = payload.get("chart")
    if not isinstance(chart, dict) or chart.get("error"):
        raise DataRejected("PROVIDER_ERROR")
    results = chart.get("result")
    if not isinstance(results, list) or len(results) != 1:
        raise DataRejected("PROVIDER_RESULT_NOT_UNIQUE")

    result = results[0]
    meta = result.get("meta", {})
    if str(meta.get("symbol", "")).upper() != symbol:
        raise DataRejected("PROVIDER_SYMBOL_MISMATCH")
    if meta.get("currency") != "USD":
        raise DataRejected("NON_USD_INSTRUMENT")

    timestamps = result.get("timestamp")
    quotes = result.get("indicators", {}).get("quote")
    if (
        not isinstance(timestamps, list)
        or not isinstance(quotes, list)
        or len(quotes) != 1
    ):
        raise DataRejected("MISSING_CANDLES")

    data = quotes[0]
    names = ("open", "high", "low", "close", "volume")
    if any(
        not isinstance(data.get(name), list)
        or len(data[name]) != len(timestamps)
        for name in names
    ):
        raise DataRejected("CANDLE_ARRAY_MISMATCH")

    bars = []
    seen = set()
    for index, timestamp in enumerate(timestamps):
        stamp = numeric(timestamp, "TIMESTAMP")
        if stamp in seen:
            raise DataRejected("DUPLICATE_TIMESTAMP")
        seen.add(stamp)

        # Use completed candles only. Never fill a missing candle.
        if stamp + 300 > end:
            continue
        bar = {"timestamp": stamp}
        for name in names:
            bar[name] = numeric(data[name][index], name.upper())

        if (
            min(bar[name] for name in ("open", "high", "low", "close")) <= 0
            or bar["volume"] < 0
            or bar["low"] > min(bar["open"], bar["close"])
            or bar["high"] < max(bar["open"], bar["close"])
            or bar["low"] > bar["high"]
        ):
            raise DataRejected("INVALID_OHLCV")
        bars.append(bar)

    bars.sort(key=lambda item: item["timestamp"])
    if len(bars) < 220:
        raise DataRejected("INSUFFICIENT_HISTORY")

    # Proposed technical-data freshness ceiling: 10 minutes after candle end.
    age = end - (bars[-1]["timestamp"] + 300)
    if not 0 <= age <= 600:
        raise DataRejected("STALE_COMPLETED_CANDLE")
    return bars, meta, age

def analyze(symbol):
    bars, meta, age = download(symbol)
    closes = [bar["close"] for bar in bars]
    price = closes[-1]
    averages = {
        period: sum(closes[-period:]) / period
        for period in (9, 21, 50, 100, 200)
    }

    today = datetime.now(NY).date()
    session = [
        bar for bar in bars
        if datetime.fromtimestamp(bar["timestamp"], NY).date() == today
    ]
    total_volume = sum(bar["volume"] for bar in session)
    if total_volume <= 0:
        raise DataRejected("SESSION_VWAP_UNAVAILABLE")
    vwap = sum(
        ((bar["high"] + bar["low"] + bar["close"]) / 3) * bar["volume"]
        for bar in session
    ) / total_volume

    preceding_volume = sum(bar["volume"] for bar in bars[-21:-1]) / 20
    if preceding_volume <= 0:
        raise DataRejected("VOLUME_REFERENCE_UNAVAILABLE")
    volume_ratio = bars[-1]["volume"] / preceding_volume

    daily_volume = defaultdict(float)
    for bar in bars:
        date = datetime.fromtimestamp(bar["timestamp"], NY).date()
        daily_volume[date] += bar["volume"]
    completed_days = sorted(date for date in daily_volume if date < today)
    # Exclude the first day: the 10-day request can begin mid-session.
    completed_days = completed_days[1:]
    if len(completed_days) < 5:
        raise DataRejected("INSUFFICIENT_COMPLETED_VOLUME_SESSIONS")
    average_volume = sum(daily_volume[date] for date in completed_days) / len(completed_days)

    fast, slow = ema(closes, 12), ema(closes, 26)
    macd = [a-b for a, b in zip(fast, slow)]
    histogram = macd[-1] - ema(macd, 9)[-1]
    rsi14 = rsi(closes)

    ranges = [
        max(
            bars[index]["high"] - bars[index]["low"],
            abs(bars[index]["high"] - bars[index-1]["close"]),
            abs(bars[index]["low"] - bars[index-1]["close"]),
        )
        for index in range(1, len(bars))
    ]
    atr14 = wilder_average(ranges)
    momentum = {
        period: (price / closes[-period-1] - 1) * 100
        for period in (5, 15, 30)
    }

    factors = {
        "ma9_above_ma21": averages[9] > averages[21],
        "ma21_above_ma50": averages[21] > averages[50],
        "price_above_ma200": price > averages[200],
        "price_above_vwap": price > vwap,
        "macd_histogram_positive": histogram > 0,
        "rsi_30_to_65": 30 <= rsi14 <= 65,
        "volume_ratio_at_least_1_5": volume_ratio >= 1.5,
        "momentum_5_positive": momentum[5] > 0,
    }
    weights = {
        "ma9_above_ma21": 2,
        "ma21_above_ma50": 2,
        "price_above_ma200": 1,
        "price_above_vwap": 1,
        "macd_histogram_positive": 1,
        "rsi_30_to_65": 1,
        "volume_ratio_at_least_1_5": 1,
        "momentum_5_positive": 1,
    }
    score = sum(weights[key] for key, passed in factors.items() if passed)

    return {
        "symbol": symbol,
        "strategy_version": VERSION,
        "state": "TECHNICAL_DATA_READY",
        "source": "YAHOO_CHART",
        "instrument_type": meta.get("instrumentType"),
        "candle_interval": "5m",
        "completed_candle_start": datetime.fromtimestamp(
            bars[-1]["timestamp"], timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
        "candle_end_age_seconds": age,
        "sample_count": len(bars),
        "price": price,
        "moving_averages": averages,
        "vwap": vwap,
        "rsi14": rsi14,
        "previous_rsi14": rsi(closes[:-1]),
        "previous_close": closes[-2],
        "atr14": atr14,
        "atr_pct": atr14/price*100,
        "macd_histogram": histogram,
        "volume_ratio": volume_ratio,
        "average_daily_volume": average_volume,
        "momentum_pct": momentum,
        "technical_score": score,
        "technical_score_max": 10,
        "factors": factors,
        "technical_rally_proposal": "STRONG" if score >= 6 else "WEAK",
        "price_and_volume_filters_pass": price >= 0.5 and average_volume >= 100000,
        "intelligence_score": None,
        "hybrid_score": None,
        "classification": "AWAITING_INTELLIGENCE_AND_RISK_CHECKS",
        "entry_authorized": False,
        "orders_submitted": False,
        "missing_gates": [
            "Verified news and earnings risk",
            "Market-regime intelligence",
            "Instrument eligibility including leveraged/inverse exclusion",
            "Live capital and reservation authorization",
            "Per-ticker daily-loss authorization",
            "Broker pre-trade review and execution integration",
        ],
    }

def self_test():
    assert rsi(list(range(1, 231))) == 100
    assert rsi(list(range(230, 0, -1))) == 0
    assert rsi([100]*230) == 50
    assert len(ema(list(range(1, 231)), 50)) == 230
    assert ema([100]*230, 50)[-1] == 100
    print(json.dumps({"unit_checks_passed": 5, "broker_calls": 0}))

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("symbols", nargs="*")
    parser.add_argument("--self-test", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.self_test:
        self_test()
        return
    if not args.symbols:
        parser.error("Provide explicit ticker symbols.")
    symbols = list(dict.fromkeys(symbol.upper() for symbol in args.symbols))
    for symbol in symbols:
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z][A-Z0-9.-]{0,9}", symbol):
            raise SystemExit("Invalid ticker format.")
    for index, symbol in enumerate(symbols):
        if index:
            time.sleep(2)
        try:
            result = analyze(symbol)
        except Exception as error:
            result = {
                "symbol": symbol,
                "strategy_version": VERSION,
                "state": "DATA_REJECTED",
                "error_type": type(error).__name__,
                "reason": str(error) if isinstance(error, DataRejected) else "PROVIDER_OR_PARSING_FAILURE",
                "entry_authorized": False,
                "orders_submitted": False,
            }
        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, allow_nan=False))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================
FILE: /opt/si-equity-r1/equity_commands_v1.py
SHA256: 242238bbc4e549ba16efd3f341baafa7eb9801e1b36446477cb185a7e66e8171
"""Equity command persistence.

Importing this module does not open a database, change permissions,
or submit orders. The dashboard and worker must explicitly call it.
"""
import json
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import UUID

DB = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-stop-guard/dashboard-commands.sqlite3")
FIELDS = {"bot", "auto_trade", "buy", "sell"}


def now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def connect():
    db = sqlite3.connect(DB, timeout=10)
    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    db.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=FULL")
    return db


def initialize():
    db = connect()
    try:
        db.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS equity_commands(
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                request_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                requested_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                updates TEXT NOT NULL,
                state TEXT NOT NULL,
                completed_at TEXT,
                worker_pid INTEGER,
                result TEXT
            );
        """)
        db.commit()
    finally:
        db.close()


def validated_request(body):
    if (
        not isinstance(body, dict)
        or set(body) != {"bot", "updates", "request_id"}
        or body["bot"] != "equity"
    ):
        raise ValueError("Require an Equity command.")

    request_id = body["request_id"]
    if not isinstance(request_id, str):
        raise ValueError("Invalid request_id.")
    UUID(request_id)

    updates = body["updates"]
    if (
        not isinstance(updates, dict)
        or not updates
        or not set(updates) <= FIELDS
        or any(type(value) is not bool for value in updates.values())
    ):
        raise ValueError("Require Boolean control values.")

    pass

    return request_id, json.dumps(
        updates, sort_keys=True, allow_nan=False
    )


def queue(body):
    request_id, payload = validated_request(body)
    db = connect()
    try:
        db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        saved = db.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM equity_commands WHERE request_id=?",
            (request_id,),
        ).fetchone()

        if saved is not None:
            if saved["updates"] != payload:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Request ID already used for another command."
                )
            command_id = saved["id"]
            state = saved["state"]
        else:
            cursor = db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO equity_commands("
                "request_id,requested_at,updates,state"
                ") VALUES(?,?,?,'QUEUED')",
                (request_id, now(), payload),
            )
            command_id = cursor.lastrowid
            state = "QUEUED"

        db.commit()
        return {
            "bot": "equity",
            "request_id": request_id,
            "command_id": command_id,
            "command_state": state,
            "worker_applied": state == "APPLIED",
            "order_submitted_by_this_http_request": False,
        }
    except Exception:
        db.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        db.close()


def history():
    db = connect()
    try:
        rows = db.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM equity_commands ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 30"
        ).fetchall()
        return [
            {
                **dict(row),
                "updates": json.loads(row["updates"]),
                "result": (
                    json.loads(row["result"])
                    if row["result"] is not None else None
                ),
            }
            for row in rows
        ]
    finally:
        db.close()


# EQUITY_COMMAND_WORKER_V1
def initialize_worker():
    initialize()
    db = connect()
    try:
        db.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS equity_control_state(
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1),
                controls TEXT NOT NULL,
                acknowledged_at TEXT,
                worker_pid INTEGER
            )
        """)
        # Preserve the existing guard's sell permissions.
        # This does not authorize automatic buying.
        initial = {
            "bot": True,
            "auto_trade": True,
            "buy": False,
            "sell": True,
        }
        db.execute(
            "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO equity_control_state"
            "(id,controls) VALUES(1,?)",
            (json.dumps(initial, sort_keys=True),),
        )
        db.commit()
    finally:
        db.close()


def apply_pending():
    import os

    db = connect()
    try:
        db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        row = db.execute(
            "SELECT controls FROM equity_control_state WHERE id=1"
        ).fetchone()
        if row is None:
            raise RuntimeError("EQUITY_CONTROL_STATE_MISSING")

        controls = json.loads(row["controls"])
        if (
            not isinstance(controls, dict)
            or set(controls) != FIELDS
            or any(type(v) is not bool for v in controls.values())
        ):
            raise RuntimeError("INVALID_EQUITY_CONTROL_STATE")

        rows = db.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM equity_commands "
            "WHERE state='QUEUED' ORDER BY id"
        ).fetchall()

        for command in rows:
            try:
                updates = json.loads(command["updates"])
                _, payload = validated_request({
                    "bot": "equity",
                    "updates": updates,
                    "request_id": command["request_id"],
                })
                controls = {**controls, **json.loads(payload)}
                state = "APPLIED"
                result = {
                    "controls": controls,
                    "scope": "EXISTING_EQUITY_SELL_GUARD_ONLY",
                    "buy_executor_connected": False,
                }
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                state = "REJECTED"
                result = {"error": "INVALID_EQUITY_COMMAND"}

            db.execute(
                "UPDATE equity_commands SET state=?,completed_at=?,"
                "worker_pid=?,result=? WHERE id=?",
                (
                    state, now(), os.getpid(),
                    json.dumps(result, sort_keys=True),
                    command["id"],
                ),
            )

        db.execute(
            "UPDATE equity_control_state SET controls=?,"
            "acknowledged_at=?,worker_pid=? WHERE id=1",
            (json.dumps(controls, sort_keys=True), now(), os.getpid()),
        )
        db.commit()
        return controls
    except Exception:
        db.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        db.close()


def require_sell_permission():
    controls = apply_pending()
    if not (
        controls["bot"]
        and controls["auto_trade"]
        and controls["sell"]
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("EQUITY_SELL_BLOCKED_BY_WORKER_CONTROLS")


# EQUITY_DASHBOARD_ROUTING_V1
def snapshot():
    if not DB.is_file():
        return {
            "bot": "equity",
            "worker_report_fresh": False,
            "controls": None,
            "scope": "EXISTING_EQUITY_SELL_GUARD_ONLY",
            "buy_executor_connected": False,
            "reason": "WORKER_HAS_NOT_INITIALIZED_COMMAND_STORE",
            "commands": [],
        }

    db = sqlite3.connect(
        "file:" + str(DB) + "?mode=ro", uri=True, timeout=5
    )
    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    try:
        db.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
        db.execute("BEGIN")
        row = db.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM equity_control_state WHERE id=1"
        ).fetchone()
        commands = db.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM equity_commands ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 15"
        ).fetchall()
    finally:
        db.close()

    age = None
    if row and row["acknowledged_at"]:
        stamp = datetime.fromisoformat(row["acknowledged_at"])
        if stamp.tzinfo is not None:
            age = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - stamp).total_seconds()

    controls = json.loads(row["controls"]) if row else None
    valid = (
        isinstance(controls, dict)
        and set(controls) == FIELDS
        and all(type(v) is bool for v in controls.values())
        and controls["buy"] is False
    )
    return {
        "bot": "equity",
        "worker_report_fresh": valid and age is not None and 0 <= age <= 210,
        "report_age_seconds": age,
        "reported_worker_pid": row["worker_pid"] if row else None,
        "process_liveness_verified": False,
        "controls": controls if valid else None,
        "scope": "EXISTING_EQUITY_SELL_GUARD_ONLY",
        "buy_executor_connected": False,
        "settings_application_connected": False,
        "commands": [
            {
                **dict(command),
                "updates": json.loads(command["updates"]),
                "result": (
                    json.loads(command["result"])
                    if command["result"] else None
                ),
            }
            for command in commands
        ],
    }

========================================================================
FILE: /opt/si-equity-r1/market_context_v1.py
SHA256: cac4599d26272bbbacb21e12ee0e665977f69dd47705d9a9813fddd6ac664811
import argparse
import json
import re
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from urllib.parse import urlencode, quote
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

import entry_signals_v1 as technical

VERSION = "equity-context-proposal-v1"
NY = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")
BENCHMARKS = ("SPY", "QQQ", "IWM")
MAX_CONTEXT_AGE = 1200

# PROPOSED definitions where the master did not specify exact conditions:
# RISK_ON: at least 2/3 benchmark prices above their completed-session
#          50-day averages AND VIX below 25. Otherwise RISK_OFF.
# Recovery: RSI crosses above 30 and the latest completed close rises.
# Strong adverse decline: 5-session return <= -5%, OR 20-session <= -10%.
#
# Market context may be up to 20 minutes old. It is NOT an executable quote.
# Daily performance uses completed sessions, excluding today's daily bar.
# News/earnings are unknown until a verified adapter supplies them.

def daily_data(symbol):
    end = int(time.time())
    query = urlencode({
        "period1": end - 90*24*60*60,
        "period2": end,
        "interval": "1d",
        "events": "splits",
    })
    url = (
        "https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/"
        + quote(symbol, safe="") + "?" + query
    )
    request = Request(url, headers={
        "User-Agent": "SI-Equity-Context/1.0",
        "Accept": "application/json",
    })
    with urlopen(request, timeout=20) as response:
        payload = json.load(response)

    chart = payload.get("chart")
    if not isinstance(chart, dict) or chart.get("error"):
        raise technical.DataRejected("DAILY_PROVIDER_ERROR")
    results = chart.get("result")
    if not isinstance(results, list) or len(results) != 1:
        raise technical.DataRejected("DAILY_RESULT_NOT_UNIQUE")
    result = results[0]
    meta = result.get("meta", {})
    if str(meta.get("symbol", "")).upper() != symbol:
        raise technical.DataRejected("DAILY_SYMBOL_MISMATCH")

    # Do not interpret unadjusted price jumps across reported splits as returns.
    if result.get("events", {}).get("splits"):
        raise technical.DataRejected("SPLIT_REQUIRES_ADJUSTMENT_REVIEW")

    timestamps = result.get("timestamp")
    quote_rows = result.get("indicators", {}).get("quote")
    if (
        not isinstance(timestamps, list)
        or not isinstance(quote_rows, list)
        or len(quote_rows) != 1
    ):
        raise technical.DataRejected("DAILY_HISTORY_MISSING")
    closes = quote_rows[0].get("close")
    if not isinstance(closes, list) or len(closes) != len(timestamps):
        raise technical.DataRejected("DAILY_ARRAY_MISMATCH")

    today = datetime.now(NY).date()
    history = []
    seen = set()
    for raw_time, raw_close in zip(timestamps, closes):
        stamp = technical.numeric(raw_time, "DAILY_TIMESTAMP")
        date = datetime.fromtimestamp(stamp, NY).date()
        if date >= today:
            continue
        if date in seen:
            raise technical.DataRejected("DUPLICATE_DAILY_SESSION")
        seen.add(date)
        close = technical.numeric(raw_close, "DAILY_CLOSE")
        if close <= 0:
            raise technical.DataRejected("NONPOSITIVE_DAILY_CLOSE")
        history.append((date, close))
    history.sort()
    if len(history) < 21:
        raise technical.DataRejected("INSUFFICIENT_20_SESSION_HISTORY")
    if (today - history[-1][0]).days > 4:
        raise technical.DataRejected("STALE_DAILY_HISTORY")

    current = technical.numeric(
        meta.get("regularMarketPrice"), "CONTEXT_MARKET_PRICE"
    )
    current_time = technical.numeric(
        meta.get("regularMarketTime"), "CONTEXT_MARKET_TIME"
    )
    age = end - current_time
    if current <= 0 or age < -5:
        raise technical.DataRejected("STALE_OR_INVALID_CONTEXT_PRICE")
    context_fresh = -5 <= age <= MAX_CONTEXT_AGE
    context_source = "REGULAR_MARKET" if context_fresh else "STALE_REGULAR_MARKET"

    values = [item[1] for item in history]
    return {
        "symbol": symbol,
        "current_context_price": current,
        "context_timestamp": datetime.fromtimestamp(
            current_time, timezone.utc
        ).isoformat(),
        "context_age_seconds": age,
        "context_fresh": context_fresh,
        "context_source": context_source,
        "completed_sessions": len(values),
        "last_completed_session": history[-1][0].isoformat(),
        "return_5_sessions_pct": (values[-1]/values[-6]-1)*100,
        "return_20_sessions_pct": (values[-1]/values[-21]-1)*100,
        "ma50_completed_sessions": (
            sum(values[-50:])/50 if len(values) >= 50 else None
        ),
    }

def market_regime():
    observations = {}
    for index, symbol in enumerate((*BENCHMARKS, "^VIX")):
        if index:
            time.sleep(2)
        observations[symbol] = daily_data(symbol)

    if any(
        observations[symbol]["ma50_completed_sessions"] is None
        for symbol in BENCHMARKS
    ):
        raise technical.DataRejected("INSUFFICIENT_BENCHMARK_HISTORY")

    above = sum(
        observations[symbol]["current_context_price"]
        > observations[symbol]["ma50_completed_sessions"]
        for symbol in BENCHMARKS
    )
    context_fresh = all(
        observation.get("context_fresh", False)
        for observation in observations.values()
    )
    vix = observations["^VIX"]["current_context_price"]
    regime = "RISK_ON" if above >= 2 and vix < 25 else "RISK_OFF"
    return {
        "rule_version": VERSION,
        "regime": regime,
        "regime_contribution": (1 if regime == "RISK_ON" else -1) if context_fresh else 0,
        "context_fresh": context_fresh,
        "benchmarks_above_ma50": above,
        "vix_context_value": vix,
        "max_context_age_seconds": MAX_CONTEXT_AGE,
        "observations": observations,
    }

def candidate_context(symbol, market):
    scan = technical.analyze(symbol)
    time.sleep(2)
    performance = daily_data(symbol)

    recovery = (
        scan["previous_rsi14"] <= 30 < scan["rsi14"]
        and scan["price"] > scan["previous_close"]
    )
    adverse = (
        performance["return_5_sessions_pct"] <= -5
        or performance["return_20_sessions_pct"] <= -10
    )
    contributions = {
        "market_regime": market["regime_contribution"],
        "five_session_return_above_2_pct": (
            1 if performance["return_5_sessions_pct"] > 2 else 0
        ),
        "twenty_session_return_above_5_pct": (
            1 if performance["return_20_sessions_pct"] > 5 else 0
        ),
        "recovery": 1 if recovery else 0,
        "volume_ratio_at_least_1_5": 1 if scan["volume_ratio"] >= 1.5 else 0,
        "strong_adverse_decline": -2 if adverse else 0,
        "news_sentiment": None,
        "near_earnings": None,
    }
    return {
        "symbol": symbol,
        "strategy_version": VERSION,
        "state": "PARTIAL_INTELLIGENCE_READY",
        "technical": scan,
        "market_regime": market["regime"],
        "performance": performance,
        "intelligence_contributions": contributions,
        "known_contribution_subtotal": sum(
            value for value in contributions.values() if value is not None
        ),
        "intelligence_score": None,
        "hybrid_score": None,
        # SI_EXPLICIT_ENTRY_BLOCKERS_V1
        "classification": "BLOCKED_INCOMPLETE_ENTRY_AUTHORIZATION",
        "missing_gates": list(dict.fromkeys([
            *scan.get("missing_gates", []),
            "Verified news-risk assessment",
            "Verified earnings-risk assessment",
            "Approved complete entry-decision policy",
            "Integrated exit ownership for newly acquired positions",
        ])),
        "entry_authorized": False,
        "orders_submitted": False,
    }

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("symbols", nargs="+")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    symbols = list(dict.fromkeys(value.upper() for value in args.symbols))
    if any(not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z][A-Z0-9.-]{0,9}", value) for value in symbols):
        raise SystemExit("Invalid ticker format.")

    try:
        market = market_regime()
        print(json.dumps({"market_context": market}, indent=2, allow_nan=False))
    except Exception as error:
        print(json.dumps({
            "state": "MARKET_CONTEXT_UNAVAILABLE",
            "error_type": type(error).__name__,
            "reason": (
                str(error) if isinstance(error, technical.DataRejected)
                else "PROVIDER_OR_PARSING_FAILURE"
            ),
            "entry_authorized": False,
            "orders_submitted": False,
        }, indent=2))
        return

    for symbol in symbols:
        time.sleep(2)
        try:
            result = candidate_context(symbol, market)
        except Exception as error:
            result = {
                "symbol": symbol,
                "state": "CONTEXT_REJECTED",
                "error_type": type(error).__name__,
                "reason": (
                    str(error) if isinstance(error, technical.DataRejected)
                    else "PROVIDER_OR_PARSING_FAILURE"
                ),
                "entry_authorized": False,
                "orders_submitted": False,
            }
        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, allow_nan=False))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: /opt/super-intelligence-dashboard/dashboard_integration_v2.py
