import json
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

EQUITY_DB = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-r1/monitor.sqlite3")
EQUITY_CONFIG = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-r1/config.json")
NATIVE_CONFIG = Path("/etc/si-crypto-r1/config.json")
OLD_FEED = Path("/home/botuser/si-bots/dashboard_live_feed.json")

def now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def age_seconds(stamp):
    try:
        parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(stamp.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        if parsed.tzinfo is None:
            return None
        return round(
            (datetime.now(timezone.utc)-parsed).total_seconds(), 1
        )
    except (AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return None

def safe_rows(rows, keys):
    if not isinstance(rows, list):
        raise ValueError("Expected broker rows.")
    return [
        {key: item[key] for key in keys if key in item}
        for item in rows[:200] if isinstance(item, dict)
    ]


# SI_EQUITY_OBSERVATION_READER_V1
# SI_PARTIAL_SETTINGS_DASHBOARD_V3
def equity_settings_observation():
    from decimal import Decimal
    result = {
        "status": "MISSING",
        "fresh": False,
        "successful_observation": False,
        "worker_applied_revision": None,
        "full_strategy_revision_applied": False,
        "worker_enforcement": "NOT_VERIFIED",
        "process_liveness_verified": False,
        "broker_execution_verified": False,
    }
    try:
        path = Path("/var/lib/si-equity-stop-guard/settings-observation.json")
        with path.open("rb") as handle:
            raw = handle.read(16385)
        if len(raw) > 16384:
            raise ValueError("Report too large")
        data = json.loads(raw)
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            raise ValueError("Invalid report")

        state = data.get("state")
        loaded = state == "PARTIALLY_APPLIED"
        if state not in {"PARTIALLY_APPLIED", "FIXED_FALLBACK"}:
            raise ValueError("Unsupported report state")

        if (
            data.get("source") != "/api/v1/strategy-settings"
            or data.get("worker_applied_revision") is not None
            or data.get("full_strategy_revision_applied") is not False
            or data.get("broker_execution_verified") is not False
            or data.get("current_desired_revision_match_verified") is not False
            or data.get("observation_only") is not (not loaded)
        ):
            raise ValueError("Unexpected report semantics")

        pid = data.get("pid")
        if type(pid) is not int or pid <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Invalid PID")
        age = age_seconds(data.get("checked_at"))
        if age is None or age < 0:
            raise ValueError("Invalid timestamp")

        raw_stop = data.get("decision_stop_loss_pct")
        if not isinstance(raw_stop, str):
            raise ValueError("Invalid stop type")
        stop = Decimal(raw_stop)
        if not stop.is_finite() or not Decimal("0.1") <= stop <= Decimal("20"):
            raise ValueError("Invalid stop range")

        revision = data.get("stop_loaded_revision")
        expected_missing = [
            "profit_start_pct", "profit_goal_pct", "profit_floor_pct",
            "daily_loss_limit_pct", "max_positions",
        ]
        if data.get("not_applied_parameters") != expected_missing:
            raise ValueError("Unexpected unsupported parameters")

        if loaded:
            if (
                type(revision) is not int or revision < 1
                or data.get("applied_parameters") != ["stop_loss_pct"]
                or data.get("decision_source") != "SHARED_SETTINGS_LAST_SUCCESSFUL_LOAD"
                or data.get("worker_enforcement") != "STOP_CALCULATION_ONLY"
            ):
                raise ValueError("Invalid partial application")
        elif (
            revision is not None
            or stop != Decimal("1")
            or data.get("applied_parameters") != []
            or data.get("decision_source") != "INITIAL_FIXED_FALLBACK"
            or data.get("worker_enforcement") != "FIXED_FALLBACK_ONLY"
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid fallback")

        fresh = age <= 30
        result.update({
            "status": state if fresh else "STALE",
            "recorded_state": state,
            "fresh": fresh,
            "checked_at": data["checked_at"],
            "age_seconds": age,
            "reported_pid": pid,
            "successful_observation": False,
            "stop_setting_loaded": fresh and loaded,
            "stop_loaded_revision": revision,
            "decision_stop_loss_pct": raw_stop,
            "decision_source": data["decision_source"],
            "applied_parameters": data["applied_parameters"],
            "not_applied_parameters": expected_missing,
            "worker_enforcement": (
                data["worker_enforcement"] if fresh else "NOT_VERIFIED"
            ),
            "observation_only": data["observation_only"],
            "current_desired_revision_match_verified": False,
        })
    except FileNotFoundError:
        pass
    except Exception as error:
        result.update({
            "status": "INVALID_OR_UNREADABLE",
            "error_type": type(error).__name__,
            "error_detail": str(error)[:160],
        })
    return result

def equity_snapshot():
    config = json.loads(EQUITY_CONFIG.read_text())
    suffix = str(config["account_last4"])
    if len(suffix) != 4 or not suffix.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("Invalid equity account binding.")

    with sqlite3.connect(
        "file:" + str(EQUITY_DB) + "?mode=ro",
        uri=True, timeout=5,
    ) as db:
        db.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
        db.execute("BEGIN")
        health = db.execute(
            "SELECT at,payload FROM health WHERE id=1"
        ).fetchone()
        latest = db.execute(
            "SELECT at,summary,broker_data FROM snapshots "
            "ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
        ).fetchone()

    if latest is None:
        return {"status": "NO_BROKER_SNAPSHOT", "controls_connected": False}

    at, summary_text, broker_text = latest
    summary = json.loads(summary_text)
    broker = json.loads(broker_text)

    if summary.get("account") != "****" + suffix:
        raise ValueError("Equity snapshot account binding mismatch.")

    age = age_seconds(at)
    fresh = age is not None and 0 <= age <= 180
    portfolio = broker.get("portfolio")
    if not isinstance(portfolio, dict):
        raise ValueError("Invalid portfolio shape.")

    positions = broker.get("positions")
    orders = broker.get("orders")
    if not isinstance(positions, list) or not isinstance(orders, list):
        raise ValueError("Invalid broker row collections.")

    return {
        "status": "SNAPSHOT_FRESH" if fresh else "SNAPSHOT_STALE",
        "snapshot_at": at,
        "age_seconds": age,
        "account": "****" + suffix,
        "source": "equity-monitor-broker-snapshot",
        "settings_observation": equity_settings_observation(),
        "controls_connected": False,
        "automatic_entries_implemented": False,
        "monitor_health": json.loads(health[1]).get("state")
            if health else None,
        "portfolio": {
            key: portfolio[key] for key in (
                "currency", "buying_power", "cash", "total_value",
                "equity_value", "crypto_value",
            ) if key in portfolio
        },
        "position_rows_total": len(positions),
        "order_rows_total": len(orders),
        "display_row_limit": 200,
        "positions": safe_rows(positions, (
            "symbol", "quantity", "shares_available_for_sells",
            "average_buy_price",
        )),
        "orders": safe_rows(orders, (
            "id", "symbol", "side", "state",
            "cumulative_quantity", "average_price", "fees",
            "created_at", "last_transaction_at",
        )),
    }

def filter_legacy_feed(payload, native_suffix):
    import ast
    import hashlib

    def digest(value):
        return hashlib.sha256(str(value).encode()).hexdigest()

    if not isinstance(payload, dict) or not isinstance(payload.get("bots"), dict):
        raise ValueError("Invalid collector payload.")
    native_account = str(json.loads(NATIVE_CONFIG.read_text())["account_number"])
    collector = Path("/home/botuser/si-bots/dashboard_live_feed.py")
    tree = ast.parse(collector.read_text())
    mappings = [node for node in tree.body if isinstance(node, ast.Assign)
                and any(isinstance(target, ast.Name) and target.id == "TARGETS" for target in node.targets)]
    if len(mappings) != 1:
        raise ValueError("Collector target mapping is not unique.")
    required = str(ast.literal_eval(mappings[0].value)["crypto"])
    result = dict(payload)
    bots = dict(payload["bots"])
    crypto = bots.get("crypto")
    proof = crypto.get("account_link", {}) if isinstance(crypto, dict) else {}
    proof_age = age_seconds(proof.get("verified_at"))
    verified = (
        proof.get("verified") is True
        and proof.get("crypto_identifier_sha256") == digest(native_account)
        and proof.get("brokerage_identifier_sha256") == digest(required)
        and proof.get("crypto_last4") == native_account[-4:]
        and proof.get("brokerage_last4") == required[-4:]
        and str(native_suffix) == native_account[-4:]
        and isinstance(crypto, dict)
        and crypto.get("account") == "****" + required[-4:]
        and proof_age is not None and 0 <= proof_age <= 300
    )
    if not verified:
        bots.pop("crypto", None)
        models = result.get("accounting_v4")
        if isinstance(models, dict):
            models = dict(models)
            models["bots"] = {k:v for k,v in models.get("bots", {}).items() if k != "crypto"}
            result["accounting_v4"] = models
    result["bots"] = bots
    result["crypto_account_binding_warning"] = None if verified else "CRYPTO_FULL_ACCOUNT_LINK_NOT_YET_VERIFIED_OR_STALE"
    result["crypto_account_link"] = {
        "verified": bool(verified), "verified_at": proof.get("verified_at"),
        "brokerage_account": "****" + required[-4:],
        "crypto_account": "****" + native_account[-4:],
    }
    result["account_suffix_check_is_not_full_identity_verification"] = False
    return result

def integration_snapshot():
    from strategy_settings_v1 import TOKEN
    import worker_controls_v1
    from crypto_worker_view import snapshot as runtime_snapshot

    result = {
        "checked_at": now(),
        "csrf": TOKEN,
        "crypto": None,
        "equity": None,
        "zerodha": {
            "status": "RUNTIME_ADAPTER_NOT_CONNECTED",
            "controls_connected": False,
        },
        "manual_orders_connected": False,
    }

    try:
        control = worker_controls_v1.snapshot()
        runtime = runtime_snapshot()
        control["runtime"] = {
            key: runtime.get(key) for key in (
                "account", "process", "heartbeat_fresh",
                "display_state", "configuration",
            )
        }
        result["crypto"] = control
    except Exception as error:
        result["crypto"] = {
            "status": "RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE",
            "error_type": type(error).__name__,
        }

    try:
        result["equity"] = equity_snapshot()
    except Exception as error:
        result["equity"] = {
            "status": "BROKER_SNAPSHOT_UNAVAILABLE",
            "error_type": type(error).__name__,
            "controls_connected": False,
        }

    return result

def handle(handler):
    from strategy_settings_v1 import secure_request, send

    path = urlsplit(handler.path).path
    if path not in {"/api/v1/integration-v2", "/api/v1/live"}:
        return False

    if handler.command != "GET":
        send(handler, 405, {"error": "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED"})
        return True

    if not secure_request(handler):
        send(handler, 403, {
            "error": "LOCAL_SAME_ORIGIN_ACCESS_REQUIRED",
            "message": "Use the existing localhost SSH tunnel.",
        })
        return True

    try:
        if path == "/api/v1/integration-v2":
            payload = integration_snapshot()
        else:
            native = json.loads(NATIVE_CONFIG.read_text())
            suffix = str(native["account_number"])[-4:]
            payload = filter_legacy_feed(
                json.loads(OLD_FEED.read_text()), suffix
            )
        send(handler, 200, payload)
    except Exception as error:
        send(handler, 503, {
            "error": "INTEGRATION_DATA_UNAVAILABLE",
            "error_type": type(error).__name__,
        })
    return True


# SI_ISOLATED_EQUITY_BUY_BRIDGE_V1
_si_equity_buy_previous_snapshot = integration_snapshot
def integration_snapshot():
    result = _si_equity_buy_previous_snapshot()
    try:
        from equity_buy_bridge_v1 import snapshot as buy_snapshot
        if not isinstance(result.get("equity"), dict):
            result["equity"] = {}
        result["equity"]["buy_worker"] = buy_snapshot()
    except Exception as exc:
        result.setdefault("equity", {})["buy_worker"] = {
            "status": "UNAVAILABLE", "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
            "worker_report_fresh": False, "buy_on_available": False
        }
    return result

FILE: /opt/super-intelligence-dashboard/equity_buy_bridge_v1.py

"""Isolated buy-worker bridge. No DB writes or broker calls on import/GET."""
import importlib.util
import json
import os
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from uuid import UUID

CONFIG=Path('/etc/si-equity-buy-worker/config.json')
ROOT=Path('/var/lib/si-equity-buy-worker')
CODE=Path('/opt/si-equity-buy-worker')
FIELDS={'bot','auto_trade','buy','sell'}

def read_json(path, limit=262144):
    with Path(path).open('rb') as f:
        raw=f.read(limit+1)
    if len(raw)>limit: raise ValueError('JSON_TOO_LARGE')
    value=json.loads(raw)
    if not isinstance(value,dict): raise ValueError('JSON_OBJECT_REQUIRED')
    return value

def elapsed(stamp):
    d=datetime.fromisoformat(stamp.replace('Z','+00:00'))
    if d.tzinfo is None: raise ValueError('TIMEZONE_REQUIRED')
    return (datetime.now(timezone.utc)-d).total_seconds()

def load(path,name):
    import sys
    spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name,path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError("MODULE_SPEC_UNAVAILABLE:"+str(path))
    module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    missing=object()
    previous=sys.modules.get(name,missing)
    sys.modules[name]=module
    try:
        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    except BaseException:
        if previous is missing:
            sys.modules.pop(name,None)
        else:
            sys.modules[name]=previous
        raise
    return module

def config():
    cfg=read_json(CONFIG)
    required={'state_directory':str(ROOT),
      'controls_database':str(ROOT/'controls.sqlite3'),
      'controls_module_path':str(CODE/'equity_commands_v1.py'),
      'funding_authorization_file':str(ROOT/'funding-authorization.json'),
      'universe_file':str(ROOT/'universe.json'),
      'broker_monitor_path':'/opt/si-equity-r1/broker_monitor.py',
      'settings_origin':'http://127.0.0.1:8000',
      'legacy_guard_database':'/var/lib/si-equity-stop-guard/guard.sqlite3'}
    if any(cfg.get(k)!=v for k,v in required.items()):
        raise ValueError('ISOLATED_CONFIGURATION_MISMATCH')
    for path in (CONFIG,ROOT,CODE,ROOT/'controls.sqlite3',CODE/'equity_commands_v1.py',ROOT/'funding-authorization.json',ROOT/'universe.json'):
        if path.is_symlink() or path.resolve()!=path:
            raise ValueError('ISOLATED_PATH_MUST_NOT_BE_A_SYMLINK')
    account=cfg.get('account_number')
    if not isinstance(account,str) or not account or '*' in account:
        raise ValueError('FULL_ACCOUNT_BINDING_REQUIRED')
    return cfg

def process_matches(pid):
    if type(pid) is not int or pid<=0: return False
    try:
        args=Path('/proc',str(pid),'cmdline').read_bytes().split(b'\0')
        return (str(CODE/'equity_worker.py').encode() in args
          and any(args[i]==b'--config' and args[i+1]==str(CONFIG).encode()
                  for i in range(len(args)-1)))
    except OSError: return False

def runtime():
    result={'worker_report_fresh':False,'process_liveness_verified':False,
      'controls':None,'commands':[],'worker_status':None}
    status=read_json(ROOT/'status.json')
    with sqlite3.connect((ROOT/'controls.sqlite3').as_uri()+'?mode=ro',uri=True,timeout=5) as db:
        db.row_factory=sqlite3.Row
        db.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON'); db.execute('BEGIN')
        state=db.execute('SELECT * FROM equity_control_state WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        presence=db.execute("SELECT * FROM equity_worker_presence WHERE role='buy_worker'").fetchone()
        rows=db.execute('SELECT * FROM equity_commands ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 15').fetchall()
    flags=json.loads(state['controls']) if state else None
    valid=isinstance(flags,dict) and set(flags)==FIELDS and all(type(v) is bool for v in flags.values())
    pid=status.get('pid')
    live=process_matches(pid)
    fresh=(live and valid and state is not None and presence is not None
      and state['worker_pid']==pid and presence['pid']==pid
      and 0<=elapsed(status['heartbeat_at'])<=45
      and 0<=elapsed(presence['at'])<=45
      and 0<=elapsed(state['acknowledged_at'])<=45)
    commands=[]
    for row in rows:
        item=dict(row)
        item['updates']=json.loads(item['updates'])
        item['result']=json.loads(item['result']) if item['result'] else None
        commands.append(item)
    result.update(worker_report_fresh=bool(fresh),process_liveness_verified=live,
      controls=flags if valid else None,commands=commands,
      worker_status={k:status.get(k) for k in ('state','reason','heartbeat_at','pid','last_cycle_completed','settings_revision')})
    return result

def snapshot():
    result={'bot':'equity','scope':'ISOLATED_EQUITY_BUY_WORKER',
      'broker_execution_verified':False,'worker_report_fresh':False,
      'process_liveness_verified':False,'controls':None,'commands':[],
      'buy_on_available':False,'blockers':[]}
    try: cfg=config()
    except Exception as exc:
        result['blockers'].append('CONFIGURATION_UNAVAILABLE:'+type(exc).__name__)
        return result
    result['account']='****'+cfg['account_number'][-4:]
    for key in ('live_enabled','programmatic_execution_authorized'):
        if cfg.get(key) is not True: result['blockers'].append(key.upper()+'_IS_FALSE')
    try:
        worker=load(CODE/'equity_worker.py','si_equity_buy_policy_reader')
        policy=_policy_snapshot_without_self_http(worker,cfg)
        if worker.number(policy['allocated_capital'])!=worker.number('130'):
            raise ValueError('AUTHORIZED_ALLOCATION_CHANGED')
        if worker.number(policy['capital_utilization_pct'])!=worker.number('90'):
            raise ValueError('SAVED_UTILIZATION_MUST_BE_90_PERCENT')
        result['settings_revision']=policy['revision']
    except Exception as exc:
        result['blockers'].append('POLICY_OR_FUNDING:'+str(exc)[:120])
    try:
        worker=load(CODE/'equity_worker.py','si_equity_buy_universe_reader')
        result['universe_symbols']=len(worker.universe(cfg))
    except Exception as exc:
        result['blockers'].append('UNIVERSE_UNAVAILABLE:'+type(exc).__name__)
    try: result.update(runtime())
    except Exception as exc:
        result['blockers'].append('WORKER_REPORT_UNAVAILABLE:'+type(exc).__name__)
    if result['worker_report_fresh'] is not True:
        result['blockers'].append('BUY_WORKER_NOT_FRESH_AND_RUNNING')
    result['buy_on_available']=not result['blockers']
    return result

def preflight():
    result=snapshot()
    ignored={'BUY_WORKER_NOT_FRESH_AND_RUNNING'}
    reasons=[r for r in result['blockers'] if r not in ignored and not r.startswith('WORKER_REPORT_UNAVAILABLE:')]
    if reasons: raise RuntimeError('; '.join(reasons))

def queue(body):
    if not isinstance(body,dict) or set(body)!={'bot','updates','request_id'} or body.get('bot')!='equity':
        raise ValueError('REQUIRE_EQUITY_COMMAND')
    if not isinstance(body['request_id'],str): raise ValueError('INVALID_REQUEST_ID')
    UUID(body['request_id'])
    updates=body['updates']
    if (not isinstance(updates,dict) or not updates or not set(updates)<=FIELDS
        or any(type(v) is not bool for v in updates.values())):
        raise ValueError('REQUIRE_BOOLEAN_CONTROLS')
    cfg=config()
    if any(updates.values()):
        status=snapshot()
        if status.get('worker_report_fresh') is not True:
            raise ValueError('BUY_WORKER_NOT_FRESH_AND_RUNNING')
        if updates.get('buy') is True and not status['buy_on_available']:
            raise ValueError('BUY_ON_BLOCKED:'+ '; '.join(status['blockers']))
        if cfg.get('live_enabled') is not True or cfg.get('programmatic_execution_authorized') is not True:
            raise ValueError('EXECUTION_NOT_ENABLED_AND_AUTHORIZED')
    if not (ROOT/'controls.sqlite3').is_file():
        raise ValueError('BUY_WORKER_COMMAND_STORE_NOT_INITIALIZED')
    module=load(CODE/'equity_commands_v1.py','si_equity_buy_commands_bridge')
    module.DB=ROOT/'controls.sqlite3'
    result=module.queue(body)
    result['scope']='ISOLATED_EQUITY_BUY_WORKER'
    return result

def handle(handler):
    if urlsplit(handler.path).path!='/api/v1/equity-buy-controls': return False
    from strategy_settings_v1 import secure_request, send
    post=handler.command=='POST'
    if not secure_request(handler,post=post):
        send(handler,403,{'error':'LOCAL_SAME_ORIGIN_ACCESS_REQUIRED'}); return True
    try:
        if handler.command=='GET': send(handler,200,snapshot())
        elif post:
            if handler.headers.get('Content-Type','').split(';')[0].strip()!='application/json':
                raise ValueError('JSON_REQUIRED')
            if handler.headers.get('Transfer-Encoding'): raise ValueError('CHUNKED_BODY_REFUSED')
            size=int(handler.headers.get('Content-Length','0'))
            if not 0<size<=4096: raise ValueError('INVALID_BODY_SIZE')
            send(handler,202,queue(json.loads(handler.rfile.read(size))))
        else: send(handler,405,{'error':'METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED'})
    except (ValueError,TypeError,UnicodeError) as exc:
        send(handler,409,{'error':str(exc)[:500]})
    except Exception as exc:
        send(handler,503,{'error':'BUY_CONTROLS_UNAVAILABLE','error_type':type(exc).__name__})
    return True


# SI_EQUITY_POLICY_NO_SELF_HTTP_V1
def _policy_snapshot_without_self_http(worker, cfg):
    import sys
    backend = sys.modules.get("__main__")
    filename = getattr(backend, "__file__", None)
    expected = Path("/opt/super-intelligence-dashboard/unified_control_api.py")
    in_backend = (
        isinstance(filename, str)
        and Path(filename).resolve() == expected
    )
    if not in_backend:
        return worker.policy_snapshot(cfg)

    import strategy_settings_v1
    saved = worker.read_local

    def read_in_process(base, route):
        if base != "http://127.0.0.1:8000":
            raise ValueError("UNAPPROVED_SETTINGS_ORIGIN")
        if route == "/api/v1/strategy-settings":
            return strategy_settings_v1.read_settings()
        if route == "/api/v1/config":
            return backend.load_state()
        raise ValueError("UNSUPPORTED_INTERNAL_SETTINGS_ROUTE")

    worker.read_local = read_in_process
    try:
        return worker.policy_snapshot(cfg)
    finally:
        worker.read_local = saved

"""Dedicated screening-display UI. Does not submit trading orders."""
import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit, parse_qs
import strategy_settings_v1 as settings

DB = Path("/var/lib/si-screening-status/display.sqlite3")
BOTS = ("equity", "zerodha", "crypto")
PATHS = {"/screening", "/api/v1/screening"}

def connect():
    db = sqlite3.connect(
        DB.as_uri() + "?mode=rw", uri=True, timeout=10
    )
    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return db

def selected(value):
    if value != "all" and value not in BOTS:
        raise ValueError("Invalid bot filter")
    return value

def records(bot):
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
        db.execute("BEGIN")
        where = "" if bot == "all" else " WHERE bot=?"
        params = () if bot == "all" else (bot,)
        count = db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM screening_rows" + where,
            params,
        ).fetchone()[0]
        rows = db.execute(
            "SELECT id,bot,created_at,payload FROM screening_rows"
            + where + " ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 200",
            params,
        ).fetchall()
        output = []
        for row in rows:
            item = dict(row)
            try:
                item["details"] = json.loads(item.pop("payload"))
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                item["details"] = item.pop("payload", "")
            output.append(item)
    return {
        "bot": bot,
        "total": count,
        "rows": output,
        "limit": 200,
        "source": "DEDICATED_SCREENING_DISPLAY_STORE",
        "worker_ingestion_verified": False,
    }

def clear(bot):
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=FULL")
        db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        if bot == "all":
            cursor = db.execute("DELETE FROM screening_rows")
        else:
            cursor = db.execute(
                "DELETE FROM screening_rows WHERE bot=?", (bot,)
            )
        deleted = cursor.rowcount
        db.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS screening_clear_audit(
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                bot_filter TEXT NOT NULL,
                deleted_rows INTEGER NOT NULL
            )
        """)
        db.execute(
            "INSERT INTO screening_clear_audit"
            "(bot_filter,deleted_rows) VALUES(?,?)",
            (bot, deleted),
        )
    return {"deleted": deleted, "bot": bot}

def handle(handler):
    url = urlsplit(handler.path)
    if url.path not in PATHS:
        return False
    post = handler.command == "POST"
    if not settings.secure_request(handler, post=post):
        settings.send(handler, 403, {"error": "ACCESS_DENIED"})
        return True

    try:
        if url.path == "/screening" and handler.command == "GET":
            html = PAGE.replace("__NONCE__", settings.TOKEN)
            html = html.replace(
                "__CSRF_JSON__", json.dumps(settings.TOKEN)
            )
            settings.send(handler, 200, html, html=True)

        elif url.path == "/api/v1/screening" and handler.command == "GET":
            query = parse_qs(url.query)
            bot = selected(query.get("bot", ["all"])[0])
            result = readable_records(
                bot, query.get("before", [None])[0]
            )
            result["health"] = screening_health()
            settings.send(handler, 200, result)

        elif url.path == "/api/v1/screening" and post:
            if handler.headers.get("Transfer-Encoding"):
                raise ValueError("Chunked requests are unsupported")
            content_type = handler.headers.get(
                "Content-Type", ""
            ).split(";")[0].strip()
            if content_type != "application/json":
                raise ValueError("JSON required")
            length = int(handler.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
            if not 0 < length <= 1024:
                raise ValueError("Invalid request size")
            body = json.loads(handler.rfile.read(length))
            if not isinstance(body, dict) or set(body) != {"action", "bot"}:
                raise ValueError("Invalid request")
            bot = selected(body["bot"])
            action = body["action"]

            if action == "clear":
                settings.send(handler, 200, clear(bot))
            elif action in ("start_collector", "start_crypto_monitoring"):
                if bot != "crypto":
                    raise ValueError("Recovery action is Crypto-only")
                settings.send(
                    handler, 200, screening_recovery(action)
                )
            else:
                raise ValueError("Invalid action")
        else:
            settings.send(handler, 405, {"error": "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED"})

    except (ValueError, TypeError, UnicodeError) as error:
        settings.send(handler, 400, {"error": str(error)})
    except Exception as error:
        settings.send(handler, 503, {
            "error": "SCREENING_OPERATION_UNAVAILABLE",
            "error_type": type(error).__name__,
        })
    return True

"+numeric(d.managed_quantity));\n if(d.spread_pct!==undefined)\n  add(details,"div","Spread: "+numeric(d.spread_pct)+"%");\n if(d.symbols_screened!==undefined)\n  add(details,"div","Symbols screened: "+numeric(d.symbols_screened));\n const raw=add(details,"details");\n add(raw,"summary","Technical details");\n add(raw,"pre",JSON.stringify(d,null,2));\n}\nasync function load(older=false){\n if(busy)return;busy=true;el("error").textContent="";\n try{\n  const query=new URLSearchParams({bot:el("bot").value});\n  if(older&&cursor)query.set("before",String(cursor));\n  const data=await request({query:"?"+query.toString()});\n  if(!older){el("rows").replaceChildren();shown=0;historyMode=false;}\n  else historyMode=true;\n  \n  const visibleRows=(data.rows||[]).filter(row=>{\n   const d=row.details||{};\n   const summary=row.bot==="equity"&&!d.symbol&&\n    ["WORKER_CYCLE","BLOCKED_CYCLE"].includes(d.event_type);\n   return el("showcycles").checked||!summary;\n  });\n  for(const row of visibleRows)renderRow(row);\n  shown+=visibleRows.length;\n\n  if(!shown){\n   const row=add(el("rows"),"tr");\n   const cell=add(row,"td","No visible records in this batch. Refresh latest, include Equity cycle summaries, or load older events.");\n   cell.colSpan=10;\n  }\n  cursor=data.next_before;\n  el("older").disabled=!data.has_more;\n  el("counts").textContent=\n   data.total+" saved events · "+shown+" displayed · timestamps shown in EST (UTC-05:00)";\n  renderHealth(data.health);\n }catch(error){el("error").textContent=error.message;}\n finally{busy=false;}\n}\nasync function action(kind){\n if(busy)return;\n let bot=el("bot").value;\n let message;\n if(kind==="clear"){\n  message="Clear stored "+(names[bot]||"all bots")+" display history? "+\n   "This does not alter the trading ledger or cancel orders.";\n }else{\n  bot="crypto";\n  message=kind==="start_collector"?\n   "Start the crypto log collector? This does not start trading.":\n   "Start the crypto worker with new BUY entries paused? "+\n   "Existing SELL permissions may still allow automatic exits. "+\n   "No trading permission switches will be enabled by this action.";\n }\n if(!confirm(message))return;\n busy=true;el("error").textContent="";\n try{\n  const result=await request({method:"POST",body:{action:kind,bot}});\n  el("error").textContent=result.status||"Display history cleared";\n }catch(error){el("error").textContent=error.message;}\n finally{busy=false;}\n await load();\n}\nel("showcycles").onchange=()=>load();\nel("refresh").onclick=()=>load();\nel("bot").onchange=()=>load();\nel("older").onclick=()=>load(true);\nel("clear").onclick=()=>action("clear");\nel("collector").onclick=()=>action("start_collector");\nel("crypto").onclick=()=>action("start_crypto_monitoring");\nsetInterval(()=>{\n if(!busy&&!historyMode&&!document.hidden)load();\n},15000);\nload();\n</script></body></html>\n'


# SI_READABLE_SCREENING_V2
import logging
import subprocess
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone

_RECOVERY_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_LAST_RECOVERY = 0.0

def normalized_time(value):
    if value is None or isinstance(value, bool):
        return None
    try:
        if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
            result = datetime.fromtimestamp(value, timezone.utc)
        else:
            text = str(value).strip()
            result = datetime.fromisoformat(text.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
            if result.tzinfo is None:
                result = result.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return result.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError, OSError):
        return None

def readable_records(bot, before=None):
    if before is not None:
        if not isinstance(before, str) or not before.isdigit():
            raise ValueError("Invalid history cursor")
        before = int(before)
        if before < 1:
            raise ValueError("Invalid history cursor")

    filters, parameters = [], []
    if bot != "all":
        filters.append("bot=?")
        parameters.append(bot)

    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
        db.execute("BEGIN")

        count_where = " WHERE " + " AND ".join(filters) if filters else ""
        count = db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM screening_rows" + count_where,
            tuple(parameters),
        ).fetchone()[0]

        if before is not None:
            filters.append("id<?")
            parameters.append(before)

        where = " WHERE " + " AND ".join(filters) if filters else ""
        rows = db.execute(
            "SELECT id,bot,created_at,payload FROM screening_rows"
            + where + " ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 201",
            tuple(parameters),
        ).fetchall()

    more = len(rows) > 200
    output = []
    for row in rows[:200]:
        try:
            details = json.loads(row["payload"])
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            details = {"notice": "Stored event could not be decoded."}
        if not isinstance(details, dict):
            details = {"notice": "Stored event is not a structured object."}

        stamp = normalized_time(row["created_at"])
        if stamp is None:
            for key in (
                "last_cycle_completed", "last_attempt",
                "quote_received_at", "at", "timestamp",
            ):
                stamp = normalized_time(details.get(key))
                if stamp:
                    break

        output.append({
            "id": row["id"],
            "bot": row["bot"],
            "timestamp": stamp,
            "details": details,
        })

    return {
        "bot": bot,
        "total": count,
        "rows": output,
        "limit": 200,
        "has_more": more,
        "next_before": output[-1]["id"] if more and output else None,
        "source": "DEDICATED_SCREENING_DISPLAY_STORE",
        "scope": "Recorded events only; not proof of all-symbol screening",
    }

def service_state(unit):
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["systemctl", "show", unit,
             "--property=ActiveState", "--value"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=4,
            check=False,
        )
        return result.stdout.strip() if result.returncode == 0 else "unknown"
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return "unknown"

def crypto_observation():
    path = Path("/var/lib/si-crypto-r1/runtime.sqlite3")
    result = {
        "service": service_state("si-crypto-r1.service"),
        "state": "UNKNOWN",
        "can_start": False,
        "fresh": False,
    }
    try:
        with sqlite3.connect(
            path.as_uri() + "?mode=ro", uri=True, timeout=3,
        ) as db:
            db.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
            db.execute("BEGIN")
            values = {}
            for key in (
                "heartbeat", "status", "entries_enabled",
                "dashboard_controls_v1",
            ):
                row = db.execute(
                    "SELECT v FROM kv WHERE k=?", (key,)
                ).fetchone()
                values[key] = json.loads(row[0]) if row else None

        status = values["status"]
        if not isinstance(status, dict):
            status = {}
        heartbeat = normalized_time(values["heartbeat"])
        age = None
        if heartbeat:
            age = (
                datetime.now(timezone.utc)
                - datetime.fromisoformat(heartbeat)
            ).total_seconds()

        controls = values["dashboard_controls_v1"]
        # Require explicit paused-buy evidence before exposing start.
        paused = (
            values["entries_enabled"] is False
            and isinstance(controls, dict)
            and controls.get("buy") is False
        )
        result.update({
            "state": status.get("state", "UNKNOWN"),
            "reason": status.get("reason"),
            "heartbeat": heartbeat,
            "age_seconds": round(age, 1) if age is not None else None,
            "fresh": age is not None and -2 <= age <= 45,
            "can_start": (
                result["service"] in ("inactive", "failed") and paused
            ),
            "entries_enabled": values["entries_enabled"],
        })
    except Exception as error:
        result["observation_error"] = type(error).__name__
    return result

def screening_health():
    crypto = crypto_observation()
    latest = {}
    try:
        with connect() as db:
            db.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
            rows = db.execute(
                "SELECT bot,MAX(created_at) AS latest "
                "FROM screening_rows GROUP BY bot"
            ).fetchall()
        latest = {
            row["bot"]: normalized_time(row["latest"])
            for row in rows
        }
    except Exception:
        pass

    last_crypto = latest.get("crypto")
    feed_age = None
    if last_crypto:
        feed_age = (
            datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            - datetime.fromisoformat(last_crypto)
        ).total_seconds()

    return {
        "checked_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "collector_service":
            service_state("si-screening-crypto-bridge.service"),
        "crypto": crypto,
        "crypto_last_record": last_crypto,
        "crypto_feed_fresh":
            feed_age is not None and -2 <= feed_age <= 60,
        "latest_records": latest,
        "feed_scope": {
            "crypto": "Collector installed; freshness reported separately",
            "equity": "Ingestion not verified by this update",
            "zerodha": "Ingestion not verified by this update",
        },
    }

def screening_recovery(action):
    global _LAST_RECOVERY

    if action not in ("start_collector", "start_crypto_monitoring"):
        raise ValueError("Unsupported recovery action")

    with _RECOVERY_LOCK:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now - _LAST_RECOVERY < 30:
            raise ValueError("Wait 30 seconds before another recovery request")
        _LAST_RECOVERY = now

        if action == "start_collector":
            unit = "si-screening-crypto-bridge.service"
            if service_state(unit) == "active":
                return {
                    "status": "ALREADY_RUNNING",
                    "action": action,
                }
        else:
            observation = crypto_observation()
            if observation["service"] == "active":
                return {
                    "status": "ALREADY_RUNNING",
                    "action": action,
                }
            if not observation["can_start"]:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Crypto start refused: explicit paused BUY permissions "
                    "could not be verified. Review Worker Controls."
                )
            unit = "si-crypto-r1.service"

        completed = subprocess.run(
            ["systemctl", "start", unit],
            capture_output=True, text=True,
            timeout=20, check=False,
        )
        logging.warning(
            "Screening recovery requested: action=%s result=%s",
            action, completed.returncode,
        )
        if completed.returncode != 0:
            raise RuntimeError("SERVICE_START_FAILED_CHECK_SYSTEM_JOURNAL")

        return {
            "status": "START_REQUEST_COMPLETED",
            "action": action,
            "service_state": service_state(unit),
            "trading_permissions_changed": False,
            "notice": "Service start is not proof of healthy cycles.",
        }


# SI_RESEARCH_DISPLAY_V1
def research_snapshot(bot):
    path = Path("/var/lib/si-market-screening-v1/screening.sqlite3")
    result = {
        "available": False,
        "source": "MULTI_SYMBOL_RESEARCH_STORE",
        "scope": "Latest stored result per symbol; not execution approval",
        "rows": [],
        "counts": [],
    }
    if not path.is_file():
        result["error"] = "RESEARCH_DATABASE_NOT_FOUND"
        return result

    db = sqlite3.connect(path.as_uri() + "?mode=ro", uri=True, timeout=2)
    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    try:
        db.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
        db.execute("BEGIN")
        where = "" if bot == "all" else " WHERE bot=?"
        args = () if bot == "all" else (bot,)
        result["counts"] = [
            dict(row) for row in db.execute(
                "SELECT bot,active,state,COUNT(*) AS count FROM items"
                + where + " GROUP BY bot,active,state", args
            )
        ]
        rows = db.execute(
            "SELECT bot,symbol,active,checked,updated,state,score,detail "
            "FROM items" + where +
            " ORDER BY active DESC,checked DESC,bot,symbol LIMIT 500",
            args,
        ).fetchall()
        for row in rows:
            item = dict(row)
            raw = item.pop("detail")
            try:
                detail = json.loads(raw or "{}")
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                detail = {}
            if not isinstance(detail, dict):
                detail = {}
            research = detail.get("research")
            if not isinstance(research, dict):
                research = {}
            technical = research.get("technical")
            if not isinstance(technical, dict):
                technical = {}

            item["price"] = technical.get("price")
            item["candle_time"] = technical.get("completed_candle_start")
            item["errors"] = {
                "analysis": detail.get("error_type"),
                "policy": detail.get("policy_error_type"),
                "research": research.get("research_errors"),
            }
            result["rows"].append(item)

        result["available"] = True
        result["display_limit"] = 500
        return result
    finally:
        db.close()


_si_original_readable_records = readable_records

def readable_records(bot, before=None):
    result = _si_original_readable_records(bot, before)
    try:
        result["research"] = research_snapshot(bot)
    except Exception as error:
        result["research"] = {
            "available": False,
            "error": "RESEARCH_READ_FAILED",
            "error_type": type(error).__name__,
            "rows": [],
            "counts": [],
        }
    return result

_RESEARCH_SCRIPT = """
<script nonce="__NONCE__">
(() => {
"use strict";
let pending = false;

function cell(row, value) {
    const node = document.createElement("td");
    node.textContent = value === null || value === undefined
        ? "Not recorded" : String(value);
    row.appendChild(node);
}
function localTime(value) {
    if (!value) return "Not recorded";
    const date = new Date(value);
    return Number.isFinite(+date) ? date.toLocaleString() : "Invalid timestamp";
}
async function refreshResearch() {
    if (pending || document.hidden) return;
    pending = true;
    const status = document.getElementById("research-status");
    try {
        const filter = document.getElementById("bot").value;
        const response = await fetch(
            "/api/v1/screening?bot=" + encodeURIComponent(filter),
            {credentials:"same-origin",cache:"no-store",
             signal:AbortSignal.timeout(30000)}
        );
        if (!response.ok) throw Error("HTTP " + response.status);
        const payload = await response.json();
        if (document.getElementById("bot").value !== filter) return;
        const data = payload.research;
        if (!data || !data.available)
            throw Error(data?.error || "Research data unavailable");

        const target = document.getElementById("research-rows");
        target.replaceChildren();

        const groups = (data.counts || []).map(item =>
            item.bot + " / " + item.state +
            (item.active ? " / active" : " / inactive") +
            ": " + item.count
        );
        status.textContent = groups.length
            ? groups.join(" · ")
            : "No research items stored for the selected bot.";

        for (const item of data.rows || []) {
            const row = document.createElement("tr");
            cell(row, localTime(item.updated));
            cell(row, item.bot);
            cell(row, item.symbol);
            cell(row, item.active ? "Yes" : "No");
            cell(row, item.state);
            cell(row, item.score);
            cell(row, item.price);
            cell(row, JSON.stringify(item.errors));
            target.appendChild(row);
        }
    } catch (error) {
        status.textContent = "Research refresh failed: " + error.message +
            ". Previously displayed rows, if any, may be stale.";
    } finally {
        pending = false;
    }
}
document.getElementById("bot").addEventListener("change", refreshResearch);
document.getElementById("refresh").addEventListener("click", refreshResearch);
setInterval(refreshResearch, 30000);
refreshResearch();
})();
</script>
"""

# SI_SELECTIVE_EVENT_DELETE_BASH_V1
_before_selected_log_delete = handle

def _delete_selected_display_logs(bot, ids):
    with connect() as db:
        db.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=FULL")
        db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        marks = ",".join("?" for _ in ids)
        suffix = "" if bot == "all" else " AND bot=?"
        args = tuple(ids) + (() if bot == "all" else (bot,))
        matches = [
            int(row[0]) for row in db.execute(
                "SELECT id FROM screening_rows WHERE id IN (" + marks + ")"
                + suffix, args
            )
        ]
        db.execute(
            "DELETE FROM screening_rows WHERE id IN (" + marks + ")"
            + suffix, args
        )
        db.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS screening_selected_delete_audit(
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                bot_filter TEXT NOT NULL,
                requested_ids TEXT NOT NULL,
                deleted_ids TEXT NOT NULL,
                deleted_rows INTEGER NOT NULL
            )
        """)
        db.execute("""
            INSERT INTO screening_selected_delete_audit
            (bot_filter,requested_ids,deleted_ids,deleted_rows)
            VALUES(?,?,?,?)
        """, (bot, json.dumps(ids), json.dumps(matches), len(matches)))
    return {"deleted":len(matches),"requested":len(ids),
            "scope":"DISPLAY_EVENTS_ONLY"}

def handle(handler):
    if urlsplit(handler.path).path != "/api/v1/screening/delete-selected":
        return _before_selected_log_delete(handler)
    if handler.command != "POST":
        settings.send(handler,405,{"error":"METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED"})
        return True
    if not settings.secure_request(handler,post=True):
        settings.send(handler,403,{"error":"ACCESS_DENIED"})
        return True
    try:
        if handler.headers.get("Transfer-Encoding"):
            raise ValueError("Unsupported encoding")
        content_type = handler.headers.get(
            "Content-Type",""
        ).split(";")[0].strip()
        if content_type != "application/json":
            raise ValueError("JSON required")
        length = int(handler.headers.get("Content-Length","0"))
        if not 0 < length <= 32768:
            raise ValueError("Invalid size")
        body = json.loads(handler.rfile.read(length))
        if not isinstance(body,dict) or set(body) != {"bot","ids"}:
            raise ValueError("Invalid body")
        bot = selected(body["bot"])
        ids = body["ids"]
        if not isinstance(ids,list) or not 1 <= len(ids) <= 500:
            raise ValueError("Select 1 to 500 logs")
        if any(
            type(i) is not int or not 1 <= i <= 9223372036854775807
            for i in ids
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid IDs")
        if len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
            raise ValueError("Duplicate IDs")
        result = _delete_selected_display_logs(bot,ids)
    except (ValueError,TypeError,UnicodeError):
        settings.send(handler,400,{"error":"INVALID_LOG_SELECTION"})
        return True
    except Exception as error:
        settings.send(handler,503,{
            "error":"LOG_DELETE_FAILED",
            "error_type":type(error).__name__
        })
        return True
    settings.send(handler,200,result)
    return True

_LOG_SELECTION_PAGE_CHANGES = [('const el=id=>document.getElementById(id);', 'const el=id=>document.getElementById(id);\n\nconst selectedLogIds=new Set();\nfunction updateLogSelection(){\n const boxes=[...document.querySelectorAll(".log-select")];\n for(const box of boxes)box.checked=selectedLogIds.has(Number(box.value));\n const master=el("select-visible-logs");\n master.checked=boxes.length>0&&boxes.every(b=>b.checked);\n master.indeterminate=boxes.some(b=>b.checked)&&!master.checked;\n el("selected-log-count").textContent=selectedLogIds.size+" selected";\n el("delete-selected-logs").disabled=busy||!selectedLogIds.size;\n}\nasync function removeLogs(clearAll=false){\n if(busy||(!clearAll&&!selectedLogIds.size))return;\n const ids=[...selectedLogIds];\n if(!clearAll&&ids.length>500){\n  alert("Select at most 500 logs per deletion.");return;\n }\n const message=clearAll\n  ?"Delete ALL stored display-event logs for ALL THREE BOTS, regardless of filter?"\n  :"Delete "+ids.length+" selected display-event logs?";\n if(!confirm(message+"\\n\\nPermanent deletion. Research results, trade ledgers, broker orders, system logs and permissions remain unchanged. New events may immediately arrive."))return;\n busy=true;updateLogSelection();el("error").textContent="";\n let success=false;\n try{\n  let result;\n  if(clearAll){\n   result=await request({\n    method:"POST",body:{action:"clear",bot:"all"}\n   });\n  }else{\n   const response=await fetch("/api/v1/screening/delete-selected",{\n    method:"POST",credentials:"same-origin",cache:"no-store",\n    signal:AbortSignal.timeout(30000),\n    headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json","X-SI-CSRF":csrf},\n    body:JSON.stringify({bot:el("bot").value,ids})\n   });\n   result=await response.json();\n   if(!response.ok)throw Error(result.error||"HTTP "+response.status);\n  }\n  selectedLogIds.clear();success=true;\n  alert(result.deleted+" display-event logs deleted.");\n }catch(error){\n  el("error").textContent=error.message+\n   ". If the request timed out, refresh to verify before retrying.";\n }finally{\n  busy=false;updateLogSelection();\n }\n if(success)await load();\n}\n'), ('<button id="clear">Clear filtered history</button>', '<button id="clear">Clear filtered history</button><button id="delete-selected-logs" disabled>Delete selected</button><button id="clear-all-logs">Clear all event logs</button><span id="selected-log-count">0 selected</span>'), ('<th>Timestamp</th><th>Bot / instrument</th>', '<th><input type="checkbox" id="select-visible-logs" aria-label="Select all displayed event logs"></th><th>Timestamp</th><th>Bot / instrument</th>'), ('const tr=add(el("rows"),"tr");', 'const tr=add(el("rows"),"tr");\n const box=add(add(tr,"td"),"input");\n box.type="checkbox";box.className="log-select";\n box.value=String(row.id);\n box.checked=selectedLogIds.has(Number(row.id));\n box.setAttribute("aria-label","Select event "+row.id);\n box.onchange=()=>{\n  const id=Number(row.id);\n  if(box.checked)selectedLogIds.add(id);else selectedLogIds.delete(id);\n  updateLogSelection();\n };'), ('if(!older){el("rows").replaceChildren();shown=0;historyMode=false;}', 'if(!older){selectedLogIds.clear();el("rows").replaceChildren();shown=0;historyMode=false;}'), ('shown+=visibleRows.length;', 'shown+=visibleRows.length;updateLogSelection();'), ('cell.colSpan=10;', 'cell.colSpan=11;'), ('if(!busy&&!historyMode&&!document.hidden)load();', 'if(!busy&&!historyMode&&!document.hidden&&!selectedLogIds.size)load();'), ('el("clear").onclick=()=>action("clear");', 'el("clear").onclick=()=>action("clear");\nel("delete-selected-logs").onclick=()=>removeLogs(false);\nel("clear-all-logs").onclick=()=>removeLogs(true);\nel("select-visible-logs").onchange=event=>{\n for(const box of document.querySelectorAll(".log-select")){\n  const id=Number(box.value);\n  if(event.target.checked)selectedLogIds.add(id);\n  else selectedLogIds.delete(id);\n }\n updateLogSelection();\n};')]
for _old,_new in _LOG_SELECTION_PAGE_CHANGES:
    if PAGE.count(_old) != 1:
        raise RuntimeError("LOG_SELECTION_FRONTEND_MISMATCH")
    PAGE = PAGE.replace(_old,_new,1)


# SI_VISIBLE_LOG_SELECTION_V2

_visible_row_anchor = 'box.setAttribute("aria-label","Select event "+row.id);'
if PAGE.count(_visible_row_anchor) != 1:
    raise RuntimeError("SELECTION_ROW_ANCHOR_MISMATCH")

PAGE = PAGE.replace(
    _visible_row_anchor,
    _visible_row_anchor +
    '\n box.insertAdjacentText("afterend"," Select");',
    1,
)

PAGE = PAGE.replace(
    'aria-label="Select all displayed event logs"></th>',
    'aria-label="Select all displayed event logs"> Select</th>',
    1,
)

# SI_RESEARCH_SELECTION_V1
_before_research_selection_handle = handle

def handle(handler):
    if urlsplit(handler.path).path != "/api/v1/screening/research-delete":
        return _before_research_selection_handle(handler)
    if handler.command != "POST":
        settings.send(handler,405,{"error":"METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED"})
        return True
    if not settings.secure_request(handler,post=True):
        settings.send(handler,403,{"error":"ACCESS_DENIED"})
        return True

    try:
        if handler.headers.get("Transfer-Encoding"):
            raise ValueError("Unsupported encoding")
        if handler.headers.get("Content-Type","").split(";")[0].strip() != "application/json":
            raise ValueError("JSON required")
        length=int(handler.headers.get("Content-Length","0"))
        if not 0 < length <= 131072:
            raise ValueError("Invalid request size")
        body=json.loads(handler.rfile.read(length))
        if not isinstance(body,dict):
            raise ValueError("Invalid body")

        action=body.get("action")
        if action == "selected":
            if set(body) != {"action","items"}:
                raise ValueError("Invalid fields")
            items=body["items"]
            if not isinstance(items,list) or not 1<=len(items)<=500:
                raise ValueError("Select 1 to 500 records")
            pairs=[]
            for item in items:
                if not isinstance(item,dict) or set(item)!={"bot","symbol"}:
                    raise ValueError("Invalid selection")
                if item["bot"] not in BOTS:
                    raise ValueError("Invalid bot")
                symbol=item["symbol"]
                if not isinstance(symbol,str) or not 1<=len(symbol)<=128:
                    raise ValueError("Invalid symbol")
                pairs.append((item["bot"],symbol))
            if len(set(pairs))!=len(pairs):
                raise ValueError("Duplicate selection")
        elif action == "all":
            if set(body)!={"action","confirmation"}:
                raise ValueError("Invalid fields")
            if body["confirmation"]!="CLEAR_ALL_RESEARCH":
                raise ValueError("Confirmation required")
            pairs=[]
        else:
            raise ValueError("Invalid action")

        research_path=Path("/var/lib/si-market-screening-v1/screening.sqlite3")
        db=sqlite3.connect(
            research_path.as_uri()+"?mode=rw",uri=True,timeout=10
        )
        try:
            with db:
                db.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=FULL")
                db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
                db.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS research_delete_audit(
                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                        at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                        action TEXT NOT NULL,
                        selected_keys TEXT NOT NULL,
                        deleted_rows INTEGER NOT NULL
                    )
                """)
                deleted=0
                if action=="all":
                    deleted=db.execute("DELETE FROM items").rowcount
                else:
                    for bot,symbol in pairs:
                        deleted+=db.execute(
                            "DELETE FROM items WHERE bot=? AND symbol=?",
                            (bot,symbol)
                        ).rowcount
                db.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO research_delete_audit"
                    "(action,selected_keys,deleted_rows) VALUES(?,?,?)",
                    (action,json.dumps(pairs),deleted)
                )
        finally:
            db.close()
        result={"deleted":deleted,"scope":"RESEARCH_ITEMS_ONLY",
                "notice":"Scanners may recreate records during discovery."}
    except (ValueError,TypeError,UnicodeError):
        settings.send(handler,400,{"error":"INVALID_RESEARCH_SELECTION"})
        return True
    except Exception as error:
        settings.send(handler,503,{
            "error":"RESEARCH_DELETE_FAILED",
            "error_type":type(error).__name__
        })
        return True

    settings.send(handler,200,result)
    return True

_RESEARCH_SELECTION_CHANGES=[('<p id="research-status">', '<div class="toolbar"><button type="button" id="research-select-all">Select all displayed research rows</button><button type="button" id="research-deselect-all">Deselect all</button><button type="button" id="research-delete-selected" disabled>Delete selected research</button><button type="button" id="research-clear-all">Clear all research records</button><span id="research-selected-count">0 selected</span></div><p class="notice">Deleting research records removes latest results and queue entries. Scanners may recreate them. This does not delete trades or orders.</p><p id="research-status">'), ('<th>Last stored update</th><th>Bot</th><th>Symbol</th>', '<th>Select</th><th>Last stored update</th><th>Bot</th><th>Symbol</th>'), ('let pending = false;', '\nlet pending = false;\nconst researchSelection=new Set();\n\nfunction updateResearchSelection(){\n const boxes=[...document.querySelectorAll(".research-select")];\n for(const box of boxes)box.checked=researchSelection.has(box.value);\n document.getElementById("research-selected-count").textContent=\n  researchSelection.size+" selected";\n document.getElementById("research-delete-selected").disabled=\n  pending||!researchSelection.size;\n}\nfunction selectResearchRows(on){\n for(const box of document.querySelectorAll(".research-select")){\n  if(on)researchSelection.add(box.value);\n  else researchSelection.delete(box.value);\n }\n if(!on)researchSelection.clear();\n updateResearchSelection();\n}\nasync function removeResearchRows(all=false){\n if(pending||(!all&&!researchSelection.size))return;\n if(!all&&researchSelection.size>500){\n  alert("Select at most 500 records.");return;\n }\n const warning=all\n  ?"Clear ALL research records across ALL bots, regardless of filter?"\n  :"Delete "+researchSelection.size+" selected research records?";\n if(!confirm(warning+\n  "\\n\\nThis also removes those research queue entries. Scanners may recreate them during discovery. Orders, positions, trading ledgers and permissions are unchanged."))return;\n\n pending=true;updateResearchSelection();\n try{\n  const body=all\n   ?{action:"all",confirmation:"CLEAR_ALL_RESEARCH"}\n   :{action:"selected",items:[...researchSelection].map(key=>{\n      const [bot,symbol]=JSON.parse(key);return {bot,symbol};\n    })};\n  const response=await fetch("/api/v1/screening/research-delete",{\n   method:"POST",credentials:"same-origin",cache:"no-store",\n   signal:AbortSignal.timeout(30000),\n   headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json","X-SI-CSRF":csrf},\n   body:JSON.stringify(body)\n  });\n  const result=await response.json();\n  if(!response.ok)throw Error(result.error||"HTTP "+response.status);\n  researchSelection.clear();\n  alert(result.deleted+" research records deleted. Scanners may recreate them.");\n }catch(error){\n  alert(error.message+". If timed out, reload to verify before retrying.");\n }finally{\n  pending=false;updateResearchSelection();\n }\n if(!researchSelection.size)await refreshResearch();\n}\n'), ('if (pending || document.hidden) return;', 'if (pending || document.hidden || researchSelection.size) return;'), ('cell(row, localTime(item.updated));', 'const selectCell=document.createElement("td");\n            selectCell.style.cssText="background:#fff3bf;white-space:nowrap";\n            const box=document.createElement("input");\n            box.type="checkbox";box.className="research-select";\n            box.value=JSON.stringify([item.bot,item.symbol]);\n            box.style.cssText="appearance:auto;width:23px;height:23px;vertical-align:middle;margin-right:8px;cursor:pointer";\n            box.setAttribute("aria-label","Select "+item.bot+" "+item.symbol);\n            box.onchange=()=>{\n                if(box.checked)researchSelection.add(box.value);\n                else researchSelection.delete(box.value);\n                updateResearchSelection();\n            };\n            selectCell.append(box,document.createTextNode("Select"));\n            row.appendChild(selectCell);\n            cell(row, localTime(item.updated));'), ('document.getElementById("bot").addEventListener("change", refreshResearch);', 'document.getElementById("bot").addEventListener("change",()=>{\n researchSelection.clear();updateResearchSelection();refreshResearch();\n});\ndocument.getElementById("research-select-all").onclick=()=>selectResearchRows(true);\ndocument.getElementById("research-deselect-all").onclick=()=>selectResearchRows(false);\ndocument.getElementById("research-delete-selected").onclick=()=>removeResearchRows(false);\ndocument.getElementById("research-clear-all").onclick=()=>removeResearchRows(true);')]
for _old,_new in _RESEARCH_SELECTION_CHANGES:
    if PAGE.count(_old)!=1:
        raise RuntimeError("RESEARCH_SELECTION_ANCHOR_MISMATCH")
    PAGE=PAGE.replace(_old,_new,1)

# SI_SCREENING_PERFORMANCE_V3
import math as _view_math
import time as _view_time

_VIEW_RESEARCH = Path(
    "/var/lib/si-market-screening-v1/screening.sqlite3"
)
_VIEW_HIDDEN = Path(
    "/var/lib/si-screening-view/hidden.sqlite3"
)
_VIEW_PREFIX = "/api/v1/screening-view/"
_VIEW_HEALTH_CACHE = {"until": 0, "value": None}
_before_performance_handle = handle


def _view_ro(path):
    db = sqlite3.connect(
        path.as_uri()+"?mode=ro", uri=True, timeout=3
    )
    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    db.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
    return db


def _view_page(query):
    raw = query.get("page", ["1"])[0]
    if not raw.isdigit() or not 1 <= int(raw) <= 1000000:
        raise ValueError("Invalid page")
    return int(raw)


def _view_json(raw):
    try:
        value = json.loads(raw or "{}")
        return value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return {}


def _view_events(bot, page, query):
    with _view_ro(DB) as db:
        db.execute("BEGIN")
        raw_anchor = query.get("anchor", [""])[0]
        if raw_anchor:
            if not raw_anchor.isdigit():
                raise ValueError("Invalid anchor")
            anchor = int(raw_anchor)
            if anchor > 9223372036854775807:
                raise ValueError("Invalid anchor")
        else:
            anchor = db.execute(
                "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(id),0) FROM screening_rows"
            ).fetchone()[0]

        where = " WHERE id<=?"
        args = [anchor]
        if bot != "all":
            where += " AND bot=?"
            args.append(bot)

        total = db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM screening_rows"+where, args
        ).fetchone()[0]
        pages = max(1, (total+49)//50)
        page = min(page, pages)

        records = db.execute(
            "SELECT id,bot,created_at,payload FROM screening_rows"
            +where+" ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50 OFFSET ?",
            args+[(page-1)*50],
        ).fetchall()

        rows = []
        for record in records:
            detail = _view_json(record["payload"])
            rows.append({
                "id": record["id"],
                "bot": record["bot"],
                "at": normalized_time(record["created_at"]),
                "symbol": detail.get("symbol"),
                "state": detail.get("state")
                    or detail.get("event_type"),
                "reason": detail.get("reason")
                    or detail.get("decision"),
                "price": detail.get("observed_price"),
                "move": detail.get("sample_move_pct"),
                "score": detail.get("score"),
            })

    return {
        "rows": rows, "total": total,
        "page": page, "pages": pages, "anchor": anchor,
        "page_size": 50,
    }


def _view_research(bot, page):
    if not _VIEW_RESEARCH.is_file():
        return {
            "rows": [], "total": 0, "stored": 0,
            "page": 1, "pages": 1, "page_size": 50,
            "notice": "Research database not available",
        }

    with _view_ro(_VIEW_RESEARCH) as db:
        if _VIEW_HIDDEN.is_file():
            db.execute(
                "ATTACH DATABASE ? AS view_hidden",
                (_VIEW_HIDDEN.as_uri()+"?mode=ro",),
            )
            hidden_clause = """
                NOT EXISTS(
                    SELECT 1 FROM view_hidden.hidden h
                    WHERE h.bot=i.bot AND h.symbol=i.symbol
                      AND h.checked=i.checked
                      AND h.updated=COALESCE(i.updated,'')
                )
            """
        else:
            hidden_clause = "1=1"

        db.execute("BEGIN")
        base = "1=1"
        args = []
        if bot != "all":
            base += " AND i.bot=?"
            args.append(bot)

        stored = db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM items i WHERE "+base, args
        ).fetchone()[0]
        where = base+" AND "+hidden_clause
        total = db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM items i WHERE "+where, args
        ).fetchone()[0]
        pages = max(1, (total+49)//50)
        page = min(page, pages)

        records = db.execute(
            "SELECT i.bot,i.symbol,i.active,i.checked,i.updated,"
            "i.state,i.score FROM items i WHERE "+where+
            " ORDER BY i.bot,i.symbol LIMIT 50 OFFSET ?",
            args+[(page-1)*50],
        ).fetchall()

        return {
            "rows": [dict(record) for record in records],
            "total": total, "stored": stored,
            "page": page, "pages": pages, "page_size": 50,
        }


def _view_hidden_connect():
    _VIEW_HIDDEN.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
    db = sqlite3.connect(_VIEW_HIDDEN, timeout=5)
    db.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hidden(
            bot TEXT NOT NULL,
            symbol TEXT NOT NULL,
            checked REAL NOT NULL,
            updated TEXT NOT NULL,
            hidden_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            PRIMARY KEY(bot,symbol)
        )
    """)
    db.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS actions(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            action TEXT NOT NULL,
            bot_filter TEXT NOT NULL,
            affected INTEGER NOT NULL
        )
    """)
    db.commit()
    return db


def _view_research_action(body):
    if not isinstance(body, dict):
        raise ValueError("Invalid body")
    action = body.get("action")
    bot = selected(body.get("bot"))

    if action == "hide_selected":
        if set(body) != {"action","bot","items"}:
            raise ValueError("Invalid fields")
        items = body["items"]
        if not isinstance(items,list) or not 1 <= len(items) <= 50:
            raise ValueError("Select 1 to 50 rows")
        versions = []
        keys = set()
        for item in items:
            if not isinstance(item,dict) or set(item) != {
                "bot","symbol","checked","updated"
            }:
                raise ValueError("Invalid item")
            if item["bot"] not in BOTS:
                raise ValueError("Invalid bot")
            if bot != "all" and item["bot"] != bot:
                raise ValueError("Filter mismatch")
            symbol = item["symbol"]
            checked = item["checked"]
            updated = item["updated"] or ""
            if not isinstance(symbol,str) or not 1 <= len(symbol) <= 128:
                raise ValueError("Invalid symbol")
            if type(checked) not in (int,float) or not _view_math.isfinite(checked):
                raise ValueError("Invalid version")
            if not isinstance(updated,str) or len(updated)>100:
                raise ValueError("Invalid timestamp")
            key = (item["bot"],symbol)
            if key in keys:
                raise ValueError("Duplicate selection")
            keys.add(key)
            versions.append((item["bot"],symbol,checked,updated))

    elif action == "hide_filtered":
        if set(body) != {"action","bot"}:
            raise ValueError("Invalid fields")
        with _view_ro(_VIEW_RESEARCH) as research:
            where = "" if bot=="all" else " WHERE bot=?"
            args = () if bot=="all" else (bot,)
            versions = [
                tuple(row) for row in research.execute(
                    "SELECT bot,symbol,checked,COALESCE(updated,'') "
                    "FROM items"+where, args
                )
            ]

    elif action == "restore_filtered":
        if set(body) != {"action","bot"}:
            raise ValueError("Invalid fields")
        versions = []
    else:
        raise ValueError("Invalid action")

    db = _view_hidden_connect()
    try:
        with db:
            db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            if action == "restore_filtered":
                if bot == "all":
                    affected = db.execute("DELETE FROM hidden").rowcount
                else:
                    affected = db.execute(
                        "DELETE FROM hidden WHERE bot=?", (bot,)
                    ).rowcount
            else:
                db.executemany("""
                    INSERT INTO hidden(bot,symbol,checked,updated)
                    VALUES(?,?,?,?)
                    ON CONFLICT(bot,symbol) DO UPDATE SET
                      checked=excluded.checked,
                      updated=excluded.updated,
                      hidden_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                """, versions)
                affected = len(versions)
            db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO actions(action,bot_filter,affected) VALUES(?,?,?)",
                (action,bot,affected),
            )
    finally:
        db.close()

    return {
        "affected": affected,
        "scope": "DISPLAY_VISIBILITY_ONLY",
        "scanner_queue_modified": False,
    }


def _view_detail(query):
    kind = query.get("kind",[""])[0]
    if kind == "events":
        raw = query.get("id",[""])[0]
        if not raw.isdigit() or not 1 <= int(raw) <= 9223372036854775807:
            raise ValueError("Invalid ID")
        with _view_ro(DB) as db:
            row = db.execute(
                "SELECT payload FROM screening_rows WHERE id=?", (int(raw),)
            ).fetchone()
    elif kind == "research":
        bot = query.get("bot",[""])[0]
        if bot not in BOTS:
            raise ValueError("Invalid bot")
        symbol = query.get("symbol",[""])[0]
        if not 1 <= len(symbol) <= 128:
            raise ValueError("Invalid symbol")
        with _view_ro(_VIEW_RESEARCH) as db:
            row = db.execute(
                "SELECT detail FROM items WHERE bot=? AND symbol=?",
                (bot,symbol),
            ).fetchone()
    else:
        raise ValueError("Invalid kind")
    return {"found":row is not None,
            "detail":_view_json(row[0]) if row else None}


def handle(handler):
    path = urlsplit(handler.path).path

    # Disable the earlier endpoint that deleted scanner queue entries.
    if path == "/api/v1/screening/research-delete":
        if not settings.secure_request(
            handler,post=handler.command=="POST"
        ):
            settings.send(handler,403,{"error":"ACCESS_DENIED"})
        else:
            settings.send(handler,409,{
                "error":"RESEARCH_QUEUE_DELETION_DISABLED",
                "message":"Use research visibility controls instead."
            })
        return True

    if not path.startswith(_VIEW_PREFIX):
        return _before_performance_handle(handler)

    if not settings.secure_request(
        handler,post=handler.command=="POST"
    ):
        settings.send(handler,403,{"error":"ACCESS_DENIED"})
        return True

    try:
        query = parse_qs(urlsplit(handler.path).query)
        operation = path[len(_VIEW_PREFIX):]
        if handler.command=="GET":
            if operation=="events":
                result=_view_events(
                    selected(query.get("bot",["all"])[0]),
                    _view_page(query),query
                )
            elif operation=="research":
                result=_view_research(
                    selected(query.get("bot",["all"])[0]),
                    _view_page(query)
                )
            elif operation=="detail":
                result=_view_detail(query)
            elif operation=="health":
                if _view_time.monotonic() >= _VIEW_HEALTH_CACHE["until"]:
                    _VIEW_HEALTH_CACHE["value"]=screening_health()
                    _VIEW_HEALTH_CACHE["until"]=_view_time.monotonic()+15
                result=_VIEW_HEALTH_CACHE["value"]
            else:
                settings.send(handler,404,{"error":"NOT_FOUND"})
                return True
        elif handler.command=="POST" and operation=="research-visibility":
            if handler.headers.get("Transfer-Encoding"):
                raise ValueError("Unsupported encoding")
            content_type=handler.headers.get(
                "Content-Type",""
            ).split(";")[0].strip()
            if content_type!="application/json":
                raise ValueError("JSON required")
            size=int(handler.headers.get("Content-Length","0"))
            if not 0<size<=32768:
                raise ValueError("Invalid size")
            result=_view_research_action(
                json.loads(handler.rfile.read(size))
            )
        else:
            settings.send(handler,405,{"error":"METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED"})
            return True
    except (ValueError,TypeError,UnicodeError):
        settings.send(handler,400,{"error":"INVALID_VIEW_REQUEST"})
        return True
    except Exception as error:
        settings.send(handler,503,{
            "error":"SCREENING_VIEW_UNAVAILABLE",
            "error_type":type(error).__name__
        })
        return True

    settings.send(handler,200,result)
    return True
