FILE: /opt/super-intelligence-dashboard/strategy_settings_v1.py
SHA256: 8bc6d354910d7652b2550d1165832244e5348b8f4bdb987c0f064b5ccab93323
def _utilization_read_defaults(payload):
    defaulted = []
    for bot in BOTS:
        profile = payload['profiles'][bot]
        if 'capital_utilization_pct' not in profile:
            profile['capital_utilization_pct'] = 90.0
            defaulted.append(bot)
    payload['capital_utilization_defaulted_bots'] = defaulted
    payload['capital_utilization_enforcement'] = {bot: 'NOT_CONNECTED' for bot in BOTS}
    return payload
import copy
import hmac
import json
import secrets
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
DB = Path('/var/lib/si-strategy-settings/settings.sqlite3')
TOKEN = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
BOTS = ('crypto', 'equity', 'zerodha')
SCHEMA = {'stop_loss_pct': {'label': 'Stop loss % — excluding fees', 'min': 0.1, 'max': 20, 'integer': False}, 'profit_start_pct': {'label': 'Start evaluating profit exits % — net of fees', 'min': 0.1, 'max': 100, 'integer': False}, 'profit_goal_pct': {'label': 'Preferred profit goal % — not a guaranteed return', 'min': 0.1, 'max': 100, 'integer': False}, 'profit_floor_pct': {'label': 'Profit retracement floor % after exceeding this level', 'min': 0.1, 'max': 100, 'integer': False}, 'daily_loss_limit_pct': {'label': 'Daily loss limit % — denominator still requires integration', 'min': 0.1, 'max': 20, 'integer': False}, 'max_positions': {'label': 'Maximum open positions', 'min': 1, 'max': 100, 'integer': True}, 'capital_utilization_pct': {'label': 'Capital utilization % — reserve is 100 minus this value', 'min': 0, 'max': 100, 'integer': False}, 'entry_size_pct': {'label': 'Entry budget % — Equity worker; Crypto/Zerodha execution pending', 'min': 0.1, 'max': 100, 'integer': False}}
ROUTES = {'/strategy-settings', '/api/v1/strategy-settings', '/api/v1/strategy-settings/history'}

class ValidationError(ValueError):
    pass

class RevisionConflict(ValueError):
    pass

def timestamp():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

def connect():
    db = sqlite3.connect(DB, timeout=10)
    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    return db

def initialize(profiles):
    with connect() as db:
        db.executescript('\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings(\n                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1),\n                revision INTEGER NOT NULL,\n                updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n                payload TEXT NOT NULL\n            );\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit(\n                revision INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n                at TEXT NOT NULL,\n                source TEXT NOT NULL,\n                targets TEXT NOT NULL,\n                updates TEXT NOT NULL,\n                before_json TEXT NOT NULL,\n                after_json TEXT NOT NULL\n            );\n        ')
        if db.execute('SELECT 1 FROM settings WHERE id=1').fetchone():
            raise ValidationError('Settings already initialized.')
        db.execute('INSERT INTO settings VALUES(1,1,?,?)', (timestamp(), json.dumps(profiles, allow_nan=False)))
        db.execute('INSERT INTO audit VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)', (1, timestamp(), 'INITIAL_DESIRED_CONFIGURATION', json.dumps(list(BOTS)), '{}', '{}', json.dumps(profiles, allow_nan=False)))

def read_settings():
    with connect() as db:
        row = db.execute('SELECT * FROM settings WHERE id=1').fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise RuntimeError('Settings not initialized.')
    return _utilization_read_defaults({'revision': row['revision'], 'updated_at': row['updated_at'], 'profiles': json.loads(row['payload']), 'schema': SCHEMA, 'worker_integration': {bot: 'NOT_CONNECTED_TO_THIS_SETTINGS_STORE' for bot in BOTS}, 'changes_are_live': False, 'capital_settings_modified': False})

def validated_profiles(profiles, targets, updates):
    if not isinstance(targets, list) or not targets or any((not isinstance(bot, str) or bot not in BOTS for bot in targets)) or (len(set(targets)) != len(targets)):
        raise ValidationError('Select unique valid bot names.')
    if not isinstance(updates, dict) or not updates or any((key not in SCHEMA for key in updates)):
        raise ValidationError('Unknown or empty parameter selection.')
    clean = {}
    for key, value in updates.items():
        if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float, str)):
            raise ValidationError('Invalid value for ' + key)
        try:
            number = Decimal(str(value))
            rule = SCHEMA[key]
            if not number.is_finite() or not Decimal(str(rule['min'])) <= number <= Decimal(str(rule['max'])):
                raise ValidationError('Out-of-range value for ' + key)
            if rule['integer']:
                if number != number.to_integral_value():
                    raise ValidationError(key + ' must be an integer.')
                clean[key] = int(number)
            else:
                if number != number.quantize(Decimal('0.0001')):
                    raise ValidationError(key + ' supports up to four decimal places.')
                clean[key] = float(number)
        except (InvalidOperation, ValueError, OverflowError):
            raise ValidationError('Invalid numeric value for ' + key) from None
    result = copy.deepcopy(profiles)
    for bot in BOTS:
        result[bot].setdefault('capital_utilization_pct', 90.0)
    for bot in targets:
        result[bot].update(clean)
        policy = result[bot]
        if not policy['profit_start_pct'] <= policy['profit_goal_pct'] <= policy['profit_floor_pct']:
            raise ValidationError(bot + ': require profit start <= preferred goal <= retracement floor.')
    return (result, clean)

def update_settings(body):
    if not isinstance(body, dict) or set(body) != {'expected_revision', 'targets', 'updates'}:
        raise ValidationError('Require expected_revision, targets, and updates only.')
    revision = body['expected_revision']
    if type(revision) is not int or revision < 1:
        raise ValidationError('Invalid expected_revision.')
    db = connect()
    try:
        db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        row = db.execute('SELECT * FROM settings WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        if row is None:
            raise RuntimeError('Settings not initialized.')
        if row['revision'] != revision:
            raise RevisionConflict('Settings changed. Reload before saving.')
        before = json.loads(row['payload'])
        after, clean = validated_profiles(before, body['targets'], body['updates'])
        new_revision = revision + 1
        at = timestamp()
        after_json = json.dumps(after, allow_nan=False)
        db.execute('UPDATE settings SET revision=?,updated_at=?,payload=? WHERE id=1', (new_revision, at, after_json))
        db.execute('INSERT INTO audit VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)', (new_revision, at, 'LOCAL_SETTINGS_UI_OR_API', json.dumps(body['targets']), json.dumps(clean), row['payload'], after_json))
        db.commit()
    except Exception:
        db.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        db.close()
    return {'status': 'SAVED_NOT_APPLIED', 'revision': new_revision, 'targets': body['targets'], 'updates': clean, 'orders_submitted': False, 'services_restarted': False, 'capital_settings_modified': False}

def history():
    with connect() as db:
        rows = db.execute('SELECT revision,at,source,targets,updates FROM audit ORDER BY revision DESC LIMIT 30').fetchall()
    return [{'revision': row['revision'], 'at': row['at'], 'source': row['source'], 'targets': json.loads(row['targets']), 'updates': json.loads(row['updates'])} for row in rows]

def _secure_request_local_v1(handler, post=False):
    if handler.client_address[0] not in ('127.0.0.1', '::1'):
        return False
    host = handler.headers.get('Host', '')
    try:
        parsed = urlsplit('http://' + host)
        if parsed.hostname not in ('localhost', '127.0.0.1', '::1') or parsed.username is not None or parsed.password is not None or parsed.path or parsed.query or parsed.fragment:
            return False
        origin = handler.headers.get('Origin')
        if origin:
            source = urlsplit(origin)
            if source.scheme not in ('http', 'https') or source.netloc != host or source.path not in ('', '/') or source.query or source.fragment:
                return False
        elif post:
            return False
    except ValueError:
        return False
    if post and (not hmac.compare_digest(handler.headers.get('X-SI-CSRF', ''), TOKEN)):
        return False
    return True


# SI_PUBLIC_PROXY_ACCESS_V1
def secure_request(handler, post=False):
    from pathlib import Path as _Path

    host = handler.headers.get("Host", "")
    public_hosts = {"207.180.203.210", "207.180.203.210:443"}

    if host not in public_hosts:
        return _secure_request_local_v1(handler, post=post)

    # Public access must come from the authenticated local Nginx proxy.
    if handler.client_address[0] not in ("127.0.0.1", "::1"):
        return False

    try:
        expected = _Path("/etc/si-dashboard-proxy.key").read_text().strip()
        supplied = handler.headers.get("X-SI-Proxy-Key", "")
        if not expected or not hmac.compare_digest(supplied, expected):
            return False
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
        return False

    if handler.headers.get("X-SI-Authenticated-User") != "dashboard":
        return False
    if handler.headers.get("X-Forwarded-Proto") != "https":
        return False

    origin = handler.headers.get("Origin")
    allowed_origins = {
        "https://207.180.203.210",
        "https://207.180.203.210:443",
    }
    if origin is not None and origin not in allowed_origins:
        return False
    if handler.headers.get("Sec-Fetch-Site", "") not in (
        "", "none", "same-origin"
    ):
        return False

    modifying = post or handler.command not in ("GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS")
    if modifying:
        if origin not in allowed_origins:
            return False
        if not hmac.compare_digest(
            handler.headers.get("X-SI-CSRF", ""), TOKEN
        ):
            return False

    return True


def send(handler, code, payload, html=False):
    raw = payload.encode('utf-8') if html else json.dumps(payload, allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
    handler.send_response(code)
    handler.send_header('Content-Type', 'text/html; charset=utf-8' if html else 'application/json')
    handler.send_header('Cache-Control', 'no-store, private')
    handler.send_header('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff')
    handler.send_header('X-Frame-Options', 'DENY')
    handler.send_header('Content-Security-Policy', "default-src 'self'; script-src 'nonce-" + TOKEN + "'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'")
    handler.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(raw)))
    handler.end_headers()
    handler.wfile.write(raw)

def handle(handler):
    # SI_TICKER_HOLDS_V1
    # SI_TICKER_ANALYSIS_V1
    from ticker_analysis_v1 import handle as analysis_handle
    if analysis_handle(handler):
        return True
    from ticker_holds_v1 import handle as ticker_holds_handle
    if ticker_holds_handle(handler):
        return True
    path = urlsplit(handler.path).path
    if path not in ROUTES:
        return False
    post = handler.command == 'POST'
    if not secure_request(handler, post=post):
        send(handler, 403, {'error': 'LOCAL_SAME_ORIGIN_ACCESS_REQUIRED', 'message': 'Use localhost through your SSH tunnel.'})
        return True
    try:
        if handler.command == 'GET' and path == '/strategy-settings':
            page = PAGE.replace('__NONCE__', TOKEN).replace('__TOKEN_JSON__', json.dumps(TOKEN))
            _holds_link = '<p><a href="/ticker-holds">Ticker BUY / SELL holds</a></p>'
            page = page.replace("</body>", _holds_link + "</body>") if "</body>" in page else page + _holds_link
            # SI_TICKER_POPUP_V2
            from ticker_holds_v1 import render_panel
            _ticker_panel = render_panel(TOKEN)
            page = page.replace("</body>", _ticker_panel + "</body>") if "</body>" in page else page + _ticker_panel
            send(handler, 200, page, html=True)
        elif handler.command == 'GET' and path == '/api/v1/strategy-settings':
            send(handler, 200, read_settings())
        elif handler.command == 'GET' and path == '/api/v1/strategy-settings/history':
            send(handler, 200, {'history': history()})
        elif post and path == '/api/v1/strategy-settings':
            if handler.headers.get('Content-Type', '').split(';')[0].strip() != 'application/json':
                raise ValidationError('Content-Type must be application/json.')
            try:
                length = int(handler.headers.get('Content-Length', '0'))
            except ValueError:
                raise ValidationError('Invalid Content-Length.') from None
            if not 0 < length <= 16384:
                raise ValidationError('Invalid request size.')
            try:
                body = json.loads(handler.rfile.read(length))
            except (ValueError, UnicodeError):
                raise ValidationError('Invalid JSON.') from None
            send(handler, 200, update_settings(body))
        else:
            send(handler, 405, {'error': 'METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED'})
    except RevisionConflict as error:
        send(handler, 409, {'error': str(error)})
    except ValidationError as error:
        send(handler, 400, {'error': str(error)})
    except Exception as error:
        send(handler, 503, {'error': 'SETTINGS_UNAVAILABLE', 'error_type': type(error).__name__})
    return True

FILE: /opt/super-intelligence-dashboard/ticker_holds_v1.py

"""SI_TICKER_POPUP_V2: default permissions plus ticker exceptions."""
import contextlib
import fcntl
import json
import os
import re
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

ROOT = Path("/var/lib/si-ticker-holds")
BOTS = ("equity", "crypto")
SIDES = ("buy", "sell")

class HoldBlocked(RuntimeError):
    pass

class Conflict(ValueError):
    pass

def valid_symbol(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise ValueError("Ticker must be text")
    value = value.strip().upper()
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9.:-]{0,39}", value):
        raise ValueError("Invalid ticker")
    return value

def read_policy():
    raw = (ROOT / "policy.json").read_bytes()
    if len(raw) > 8388608:
        raise ValueError("Policy too large")
    data = json.loads(raw)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise ValueError("Invalid policy")

    legacy = {"revision", "holds"}
    modern = legacy | {"default_allow", "allow_overrides"}
    if set(data) not in (legacy, modern, modern | {"history"}):
        raise ValueError("Unexpected policy fields")
    if type(data["revision"]) is not int or data["revision"] < 1:
        raise ValueError("Invalid revision")

    # Migrate old holds in memory. Existing restrictions are preserved.
    if set(data) == legacy:
        data["default_allow"] = {
            bot: {side: True for side in SIDES} for bot in BOTS
        }
        data["allow_overrides"] = {
            bot: {side: [] for side in SIDES} for bot in BOTS
        }

    for key in ("holds", "default_allow", "allow_overrides"):
        if not isinstance(data[key], dict) or set(data[key]) != set(BOTS):
            raise ValueError("Invalid bot policy")
        for bot in BOTS:
            rules = data[key][bot]
            if not isinstance(rules, dict) or set(rules) != set(SIDES):
                raise ValueError("Invalid side policy")
            for side in SIDES:
                values = rules[side]
                if key == "default_allow":
                    if type(values) is not bool:
                        raise ValueError("Invalid default permission")
                elif (
                    not isinstance(values, list)
                    or len(values) > 1000
                    or any(valid_symbol(v) != v for v in values)
                    or len(set(values)) != len(values)
                ):
                    raise ValueError("Invalid ticker list")

    for bot in BOTS:
        for side in SIDES:
            if set(data["holds"][bot][side]) & set(
                data["allow_overrides"][bot][side]
            ):
                raise ValueError("Conflicting ticker overrides")
    history = data.setdefault("history", [])
    if (
        not isinstance(history, list)
        or len(history) > 20
        or any(not isinstance(item, dict) for item in history)
    ):
        raise ValueError("Invalid policy history")
    return data

def permitted(policy, bot, symbol, side):
    if symbol in policy["holds"][bot][side]:
        return False
    if symbol in policy["allow_overrides"][bot][side]:
        return True
    return policy["default_allow"][bot][side]

@contextlib.contextmanager
def gate(bot, symbol, side):
    lock = None
    try:
        try:
            if bot not in BOTS or side not in SIDES:
                raise ValueError("Unsupported scope")
            symbol = valid_symbol(symbol)
            lock = (ROOT / "policy.lock").open("rb")
            fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_SH)
            if not permitted(read_policy(), bot, symbol, side):
                raise HoldBlocked(
                    f"TICKER_{side.upper()}_HELD:{bot}:{symbol}"
                )
        except HoldBlocked:
            raise
        except Exception as error:
            raise HoldBlocked("TICKER_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE") from error

        # Broker exceptions must NOT be converted to HoldBlocked.
        yield
    finally:
        if lock is not None:
            try:
                fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
            finally:
                lock.close()

def check(bot, symbol, side):
    with gate(bot, symbol, side):
        pass

def snapshot():
    with (ROOT / "policy.lock").open("rb") as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_SH)
        return read_policy()

def save(body):
    if isinstance(body, dict) and "undo_revision" in body:
        return undo_change(body)
    required = {
        "expected_revision", "bot", "sides", "symbols",
        "action", "acknowledge_sell_risk",
    }
    if (
        not isinstance(body, dict)
        or set(body) not in (required, required | {"all_tickers"})
    ):
        raise ValueError("Unexpected or missing fields")

    bot = body["bot"]
    sides = body["sides"]
    all_tickers = body.get("all_tickers", False)

    if bot not in BOTS:
        raise ValueError("Only Equity and Crypto are connected")
    if (
        not isinstance(sides, list) or not sides
        or any(side not in SIDES for side in sides)
        or len(set(sides)) != len(sides)
    ):
        raise ValueError("Invalid side selection")
    if body["action"] not in ("hold", "release"):
        raise ValueError("Invalid action")
    if type(all_tickers) is not bool:
        raise ValueError("Invalid All selection")
    if type(body["expected_revision"]) is not int:
        raise ValueError("Invalid revision")
    if type(body["acknowledge_sell_risk"]) is not bool:
        raise ValueError("Invalid acknowledgement")
    if (
        body["action"] == "hold"
        and "sell" in sides
        and not body["acknowledge_sell_risk"]
    ):
        raise ValueError("Confirm that SELL OFF blocks protective exits")

    values = body["symbols"]
    if not isinstance(values, list):
        raise ValueError("Tickers must be a list")
    if all_tickers:
        if values:
            raise ValueError("All scope must not contain selected tickers")
        symbols = set()
    else:
        if not 1 <= len(values) <= 500:
            raise ValueError("Select 1 to 500 tickers")
        symbols = {valid_symbol(value) for value in values}

    with (ROOT / "policy.lock").open("rb") as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        data = read_policy()
        if data["revision"] != body["expected_revision"]:
            raise Conflict("Policy changed. Reload before saving.")

        before = {
            side: side_state(data, bot, side) for side in sides
        }
        allow = body["action"] == "release"
        for side in sides:
            if all_tickers:
                data["default_allow"][bot][side] = allow
                data["holds"][bot][side] = []
                data["allow_overrides"][bot][side] = []
            else:
                denied = set(data["holds"][bot][side])
                allowed = set(data["allow_overrides"][bot][side])
                if allow:
                    denied.difference_update(symbols)
                    allowed.update(symbols)
                else:
                    allowed.difference_update(symbols)
                    denied.update(symbols)
                if max(len(denied), len(allowed)) > 1000:
                    raise ValueError("Maximum 1000 overrides per side")
                data["holds"][bot][side] = sorted(denied)
                data["allow_overrides"][bot][side] = sorted(allowed)

        data["revision"] += 1
        record_change(
            data, bot, sides, body["action"],
            "all" if all_tickers else "selected",
            sorted(symbols), before,
        )
        fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".policy-", dir=ROOT)
        try:
            with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as output:
                os.fchmod(output.fileno(), 0o644)
                json.dump(data, output, allow_nan=False)
                output.flush()
                os.fsync(output.fileno())
            os.replace(temporary, ROOT / "policy.json")
            directory = os.open(ROOT, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
            try:
                os.fsync(directory)
            finally:
                os.close(directory)
        finally:
            if os.path.exists(temporary):
                os.unlink(temporary)
        return data

def render_panel(token):
    content = _SI_CONFIGURATION_NAV + PANEL
    return content.replace("__NONCE__", token).replace(
        "__TOKEN_JSON__", json.dumps(token)
    )

def handle(handler):
    path = urlsplit(handler.path).path
    if path not in ("/ticker-holds", "/api/v1/ticker-holds"):
        return False
    import strategy_settings_v1 as settings
    post = handler.command == "POST"
    if not settings.secure_request(handler, post=post):
        settings.send(handler, 403, {"error":"SAME_ORIGIN_ACCESS_REQUIRED"})
        return True
    try:
        if handler.command == "GET" and path == "/ticker-holds":
            page = (
                '<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8">'
                '<title>Ticker permissions</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="/static/dashboard-theme-ocean-blue-whatsapp.css"></head><body>'
                '<p><a href="/strategy-settings">Configurations</a></p>'
                + render_panel(settings.TOKEN) + '</body></html>'
            )
            settings.send(handler, 200, page, html=True)
        elif handler.command == "GET":
            settings.send(handler, 200, snapshot())
        elif post and path == "/api/v1/ticker-holds":
            if handler.headers.get("Transfer-Encoding"):
                raise ValueError("Chunked writes unsupported")
            content_type = handler.headers.get("Content-Type", "")
            if content_type.split(";")[0].strip() != "application/json":
                raise ValueError("JSON required")
            length = int(handler.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
            if not 0 < length <= 32768:
                raise ValueError("Invalid request size")
            settings.send(
                handler, 200, save(json.loads(handler.rfile.read(length)))
            )
        else:
            settings.send(handler, 405, {"error":"METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED"})
    except Conflict as error:
        settings.send(handler, 409, {"error":str(error)})
    except (ValueError, TypeError, UnicodeError) as error:
        settings.send(handler, 400, {"error":str(error)})
    except Exception as error:
        settings.send(handler, 503, {
            "error":"TICKER_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE",
            "error_type":type(error).__name__,
        })
    return True


# SI_TICKER_PANELS_HISTORY_V3
def side_state(data, bot, side):
    return {
        "default_allow": data["default_allow"][bot][side],
        "holds": list(data["holds"][bot][side]),
        "allow_overrides": list(data["allow_overrides"][bot][side]),
    }

def validated_side(value):
    if (
        not isinstance(value, dict)
        or set(value) != {"default_allow", "holds", "allow_overrides"}
        or type(value["default_allow"]) is not bool
    ):
        raise ValueError("Invalid historical side state")
    for key in ("holds", "allow_overrides"):
        values = value[key]
        if (
            not isinstance(values, list)
            or len(values) > 1000
            or any(valid_symbol(v) != v for v in values)
            or len(values) != len(set(values))
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid historical ticker list")
    if set(value["holds"]) & set(value["allow_overrides"]):
        raise ValueError("Conflicting historical overrides")
    return value

def put_side(data, bot, side, value):
    value = validated_side(value)
    data["default_allow"][bot][side] = value["default_allow"]
    data["holds"][bot][side] = list(value["holds"])
    data["allow_overrides"][bot][side] = list(value["allow_overrides"])

def record_change(
    data, bot, sides, action, scope, symbols, before, undo_of=None
):
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    record = {
        "revision": data["revision"],
        "at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "bot": bot,
        "sides": list(sides),
        "action": action,
        "scope": scope,
        "symbols": list(symbols),
        "before": before,
        "after": {
            side: side_state(data, bot, side) for side in sides
        },
    }
    if undo_of is not None:
        record["undo_of"] = undo_of
    history = data.setdefault("history", [])
    history.append(record)
    del history[:-20]

    # Keep bounded history within the reader's maximum file size.
    while (
        len(history) > 1
        and len(json.dumps(data, allow_nan=False).encode()) > 8388608
    ):
        history.pop(0)
    if len(json.dumps(data, allow_nan=False).encode()) > 8388608:
        raise ValueError("Policy/history exceeds size limit")

def write_policy(data):
    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".policy-", dir=ROOT)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as output:
            os.fchmod(output.fileno(), 0o644)
            json.dump(data, output, allow_nan=False)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, ROOT / "policy.json")
        directory = os.open(ROOT, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
        try:
            os.fsync(directory)
        finally:
            os.close(directory)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary):
            os.unlink(temporary)

def undo_change(body):
    if set(body) != {
        "expected_revision", "undo_revision", "acknowledge_sell_risk"
    }:
        raise ValueError("Unexpected undo fields")
    if (
        type(body["expected_revision"]) is not int
        or type(body["undo_revision"]) is not int
        or type(body["acknowledge_sell_risk"]) is not bool
    ):
        raise ValueError("Invalid undo values")

    with (ROOT / "policy.lock").open("rb") as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        data = read_policy()

        if data["revision"] != body["expected_revision"]:
            raise Conflict("Policy changed. Reload before undoing.")

        matches = [
            item for item in data["history"]
            if item.get("revision") == body["undo_revision"]
        ]
        if len(matches) != 1:
            raise ValueError("Change is no longer in retained history")

        item = matches[0]
        if item.get("undone_by") is not None:
            raise Conflict("This change was already undone")

        bot = item.get("bot")
        sides = item.get("sides")
        if (
            bot not in BOTS
            or not isinstance(sides, list)
            or not sides
            or any(side not in SIDES for side in sides)
            or len(sides) != len(set(sides))
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid historical scope")

        if (
            "sell" in sides
            and not body["acknowledge_sell_risk"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("Confirm restoration of SELL policy")

        before = {
            side: side_state(data, bot, side) for side in sides
        }
        for side in sides:
            previous = validated_side(item["before"][side])
            expected = validated_side(item["after"][side])
            if before[side] != expected:
                raise Conflict(
                    "This side changed afterward. "
                    "Use Hold/Remove instead of undoing an older state."
                )

        for side in sides:
            put_side(data, bot, side, item["before"][side])

        data["revision"] += 1
        item["undone_by"] = data["revision"]
        record_change(
            data, bot, sides, "undo", item.get("scope", "selected"),
            item.get("symbols", []), before,
            undo_of=body["undo_revision"],
        )
        write_policy(data)
        return data
